```
"""Availability-aware Pangu selection. All stored timestamps are UTC Unix seconds."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
import numpy as np

HOUR = 3600
LATENCY = 12 * HOUR
PERIOD = 12 * HOUR


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AvailabilityProtocol:
    name: str
    latency_hours: int
    period_hours: int
    maximum_lead_hours: int

    @property
    def latency(self):
        return self.latency_hours * HOUR

    @property
    def period(self):
        return self.period_hours * HOUR


TWICE_DAILY = AvailabilityProtocol('twice_daily_08_20_BJT_latency12h', 12, 12, 30)
DAILY = AvailabilityProtocol('daily_08_BJT_latency15h', 15, 24, 45)
PROTOCOLS = {p.name: p for p in (TWICE_DAILY, DAILY)}
LOCAL = ZoneInfo('Asia/Shanghai')
BAD_START = int(datetime(2024, 11, 1, tzinfo=LOCAL).timestamp())
BAD_END = int(datetime(2024, 12, 1, tzinfo=LOCAL).timestamp())
BAD_UTC_START = int(datetime(2024, 11, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc).timestamp())
BAD_UTC_END = int(datetime(2024, 12, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc).timestamp())


def unix(value: str, timezone_name: str = 'Asia/Shanghai') -> int:
    """Localize naive power timestamps; preserve timezone-aware NWP timestamps."""
    stamp = datetime.fromisoformat(value)
    if stamp.tzinfo is None:
        stamp = stamp.replace(tzinfo=ZoneInfo(timezone_name))
    return int(stamp.timestamp())


def select_issue(origin, protocol=TWICE_DAILY):
    origin = np.asarray(origin, dtype=np.int64)
    return ((origin - protocol.latency) // protocol.period) * protocol.period


def plan_times(origin, protocol=TWICE_DAILY):
    origin = np.asarray(origin, dtype=np.int64)
    if np.any(origin % 900):
        raise ValueError('Power origins must lie on the 15-minute time grid')
    issue = select_issue(origin, protocol)
    base = (origin // HOUR) * HOUR
    future = base[:, None] + np.arange(8, dtype=np.int64)[None] * HOUR
    past = base[:, None] + np.arange(-6, 0, dtype=np.int64)[None] * HOUR
    available = issue + protocol.latency
    assert np.all(available <= origin) and np.all(origin < available + protocol.period)
    assert np.all(issue % protocol.period == 0)
    assert np.all(future[:, 0] <= origin + 900)
    assert np.all(future[:, -1] >= origin + 6 * HOUR)
    assert np.all((future - issue[:, None]) <= protocol.maximum_lead_hours * HOUR)
    return dict(issue=issue, available=available, base=base, future=future, past=past)


def november_exclusions(origin, protocol=TWICE_DAILY):
    """Conservative exclusion of local power/weather windows and bad issue files.

    Covers the UTC filename month as well as the business calendar month. This
    also prevents December boundary samples from reusing a November issue.
    """
    origin = np.asarray(origin, dtype=np.int64)
    p = plan_times(origin, protocol)
    overlaps = lambda a, b: (a < BAD_END) & (b >= BAD_START)
    return {
        'power_window_touches_local_november': overlaps(origin - 23 * 900, origin + 24 * 900),
        'weather_window_touches_local_november': overlaps(p['past'][:, 0], p['future'][:, -1]),
        'issue_in_utc_november': (p['issue'] >= BAD_UTC_START) & (p['issue'] < BAD_UTC_END),
        'issue_in_local_november': (p['issue'] >= BAD_START) & (p['issue'] < BAD_END),
    }


def source_path(root: str | Path, issue: int, lead: int) -> Path:
    if lead < 0 or lead > 96:
        raise ValueError(f'Unsupported Pangu lead: {lead}')
    stamp = datetime.fromtimestamp(int(issue), tz=timezone.utc)
    step = f'{int(lead):02d}'
    return Path(root) / str(stamp.year) / step / f'{stamp:%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S}+00:00-{step}.npy'


def interpolate_nodes(nodes, offsets, queries):
    """Hourly linear interpolation without silent endpoint extrapolation."""
    nodes = np.asarray(nodes)
    offsets = np.asarray(offsets)
    queries = np.asarray(queries, dtype=np.float32)
    if queries.ndim == 1:
        queries = np.broadcast_to(queries, (len(nodes), len(queries)))
    if not np.allclose(np.diff(offsets, axis=1), 1):
        raise ValueError('Expected contiguous hourly nodes')
    x = queries - offsets[:, :1]
    if np.any(x < -1e-6) or np.any(x > nodes.shape[1] - 1 + 1e-6):
        raise ValueError('Required forecast valid time is outside available nodes')
    lo = np.minimum(np.floor(x).astype(np.int64), nodes.shape[1] - 2)
    frac = (x - lo)[..., None]
    rows = np.arange(len(nodes))[:, None]
    return (nodes[rows, lo] * (1 - frac) + nodes[rows, lo + 1] * frac).astype(np.float32)
```

```
"""Single-farm BASE/SEL inputs from the operational bundle, with provenance guards.

No path to the former local-weather cache, prepared histories, or feature stats
is used. Regional observations can be read from this same cache's history array.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import torch
from .operational import interpolate_nodes, PROTOCOLS


def _physics():
    project = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
    path = project / 'runs/regional_reliability_validation_20261027_v1/code/physics.py'
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('operational_physics_functions', path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


class OperationalFarmData:
    def __init__(self, cache_dir, split, site, feature_set='SEL', stats=None, device='cpu', limit=None):
        if split not in ['train', 'val', 'test'] or feature_set not in ['BASE', 'SEL']:
            raise ValueError('Supported splits: train/val/test; features: BASE/SEL')
        root = Path(cache_dir).resolve()
        manifest = json.loads((root / 'manifest.json').read_text())
        if manifest.get('operational_protocol', {}).get('protocol') not in PROTOCOLS:
            raise ValueError('An operational data manifest is required')
        bundle = root.parent
        if not (bundle / 'SUCCESS.json').exists():
            raise ValueError('Operational dataset completion audit has not passed')
        fingerprint = manifest['preprocess_fingerprint']
        if stats is None and split != 'train':
            raise ValueError('Validation/test must use training feature statistics')
        if stats is not None:
            if stats.get('data_fingerprint') != fingerprint or stats.get('feature_set') != feature_set:
                raise ValueError('Feature statistics are from another data protocol or feature set')
        if not 0 <= site < 56:
            raise ValueError('Site index must be 0..55')
        source = root / split
        prepared = Path(manifest['physics_dir'])
        origin = np.load(source / 'origin_unix.npy')[:limit]
        cycle = np.load(source / 'weather_cycle_index.npy')[:limit]
        offset = np.load(source / 'weather_time_offset_hours.npy')[:limit]
        h = np.array(np.load(source / 'station_history.npy', mmap_mode='r')[:limit, site])
        y = np.array(np.load(source / 'station_future.npy', mmap_mode='r')[:limit, site])
        nodes = np.load(Path(manifest['local_weather_dir']) / f'{split}.npy', mmap_mode='r')[cycle, site]
        past = np.load(prepared / split / 'past_weather.npy', mmap_mode='r')[cycle, site]
        past_time = (np.load(prepared / split / 'past_valid_unix.npy')[cycle] - origin[:, None]) / 3600
        times = np.concatenate([past_time, offset], 1).astype('f4')
        allnodes = np.concatenate([past, nodes], 1)
        lead = np.arange(1, 25, dtype='f4') / 4
        weather = interpolate_nodes(nodes, offset, lead)
        reference = interpolate_nodes(nodes, offset, np.array([0.]))[:, 0]
        mean = np.load(root / 'static/nwp_mean.npy')
        std = np.load(root / 'static/nwp_std.npy')
        physical = weather * std + mean
        ref_physical = reference * std + mean
        physics = _physics()
        elevation = float(np.load(prepared / 'elevation.npy')[site])
        density, stability = physics.thermodynamics(physical, elevation)
        ref_density, _ = physics.thermodynamics(ref_physical, elevation)
        dyn_nodes = np.load(prepared / split / 'dynamics.npy', mmap_mode='r')[cycle, site]
        dynamics = interpolate_nodes(dyn_nodes, offset, lead)
        if stats is None:
            train_h = np.load(root / 'train/station_history.npy', mmap_mode='r')[:, site]
            flat = float(np.quantile(abs(np.diff(train_h, axis=1)), .1))
        else:
            flat = stats['flat_threshold']
        state = physics.operating_state(h, flat)
        repeated = np.broadcast_to(state[:, None], (len(h), 24, state.shape[-1]))
        raw = np.concatenate([density, dynamics, stability, repeated], -1) if feature_set == 'SEL' else np.zeros((len(h), 24, 0), 'f4')
        if stats is None:
            mu = raw.mean((0, 1), dtype=np.float64).astype('f4') if raw.shape[-1] else np.zeros(0, 'f4')
            sd = raw.std((0, 1), dtype=np.float64).astype('f4').clip(1e-4) if raw.shape[-1] else np.ones(0, 'f4')
            stats = dict(data_fingerprint=fingerprint, feature_set=feature_set, fitted_split='train',
                site=site, samples=len(h), mean=mu.tolist(), std=sd.tolist(), flat_threshold=flat,
                groups=['density', 'dynamics', 'stability', 'state'] if feature_set == 'SEL' else [])
        if stats.get('site') != site or stats.get('fitted_split') != 'train':
            raise ValueError('Feature statistics must come from this farm training split')
        x = (raw - np.asarray(stats['mean'], 'f4')) / np.asarray(stats['std'], 'f4')
        assert np.isfinite(x).all()
        tensor = lambda a, dtype=torch.float32: torch.as_tensor(np.array(a), dtype=dtype, device=device)
        self.n = len(h)
        self.site, self.split, self.origin, self.stats = site, split, origin, stats
        self.h, self.y = tensor(h), tensor(y)
        self.phase = tensor(np.load(source / 'origin_phase.npy')[:limit], torch.long)
        for name, array in dict(weather=weather, reference=reference, physical=physical,
                reference_physical=ref_physical, density=density, ref_density=ref_density, x=x,
                times=offset, all_times=times, allnodes=allnodes,
                historical_input=np.concatenate([h, past.reshape(len(h), -1), past_time / 6], 1)).items():
            setattr(self, name, tensor(array))
        self.diagnostic = dict(density=tensor(density), stability=tensor(stability),
            dynamics=tensor(dynamics), state=tensor(repeated), calendar=tensor(physics.calendar(origin, lead)))
        self.data_fingerprint = fingerprint
        self.issue_unix = np.load(source / 'origin_issue_unix.npy')[:limit]
        self.available_unix = np.load(source / 'origin_available_unix.npy')[:limit]

    def batch(self, indices):
        return {key: getattr(self, key)[indices] for key in ['h', 'phase', 'weather', 'reference', 'physical',
            'reference_physical', 'density', 'ref_density', 'x', 'times', 'all_times', 'allnodes', 'historical_input']}
```

```
"""Rebuild availability-correct train/val/test caches without altering old runs.

Reads only the required 24x25 regional crop of each global, hourly Pangu file.
All power timestamps and targets retain their original UTC Unix alignment.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
import csv
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import fcntl
import hashlib
import importlib.util
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import sys
import time
import h5py
import numpy as np

PROJECT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT / 'src'))
from wind_forcing.data import operational
from wind_forcing.data.operational import source_path, LOCAL
from wind_forcing.models.components import build_station_patch_metadata

DEFAULT_ROOT = Path('/hj102_data_large/ERA5_forecasting/Pangu/Forecast_data')
DEFAULT_OUT = Path('/shared_data_ssd3/liying/operational_pangu_20261027_v1')
OLD = PROJECT / 'data/model_cache_pad5_f8'
H5 = PROJECT / 'data/jilin_56farm_pad8.h5'
PHYSICS = PROJECT / 'runs/regional_reliability_validation_20261027_v1/code/physics.py'
PROTOCOL = operational.TWICE_DAILY
REQUIRE_SAME_SAMPLES = False


def plan_times(origin):
    return operational.plan_times(origin, PROTOCOL)


def november_exclusions(origin):
    return operational.november_exclusions(origin, PROTOCOL)


def now():
    return datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat()


def digest(path):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open('rb') as f:
        for b in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b''):
            h.update(b)
    return h.hexdigest()


def atomic_json(path, value):
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temp = path.with_name(path.name + '.tmp')
    temp.write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, allow_nan=False) + '\n')
    os.replace(temp, path)


def save(path, value):
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temp = path.with_name(path.name + '.tmp')
    with temp.open('wb') as f:
        np.save(f, value, allow_pickle=False)
    os.replace(temp, path)


def mmap_output(path, shape, dtype='f4'):
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return np.lib.format.open_memmap(path, mode='w+', dtype=dtype, shape=shape)


def load_physics():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('operational_frozen_physics', PHYSICS)
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


def timestamp(t, local=False):
    return datetime.fromtimestamp(int(t), tz=LOCAL if local else timezone.utc).isoformat()


def build_plans(out, raw_root):
    plans = {}
    all_keys = set()
    exclusions = {}
    for split in ['train', 'val', 'test']:
        original = np.load(OLD / split / 'origin_unix.npy')
        reasons = november_exclusions(original) if split == 'test' else {}
        excluded = np.logical_or.reduce(list(reasons.values())) if reasons else np.zeros(len(original), bool)
        if REQUIRE_SAME_SAMPLES and excluded.any():
            raise ValueError(f'{split}: new protocol excludes {excluded.sum()} existing samples; sample changes require an explicit decision')
        keep = np.flatnonzero(~excluded)
        origin = original[keep]
        times = plan_times(origin)
        base, first, inverse = np.unique(times['base'], return_index=True, return_inverse=True)
        issue = times['issue'][first]
        future = times['future'][first]
        past = times['past'][first]
        assert np.array_equal(issue[inverse], times['issue'])
        assert np.all((origin - times['issue'] >= PROTOCOL.latency) &
                      (origin - times['issue'] < PROTOCOL.latency + PROTOCOL.period))
        for t, valid in zip(issue, np.concatenate([past, future], 1)):
            all_keys.update((int(t), int((v - t) // 3600)) for v in valid)
        plans[split] = dict(keep=keep, origin=origin, base=base, cycle=inverse, issue=issue, future=future, past=past)
        exclusions[split] = dict(original=len(original), retained=len(origin), removed=int(excluded.sum()),
            reason_counts_overlapping={k: int(v.sum()) for k, v in reasons.items()})
        if excluded.any():
            with (out / f'excluded_{split}.csv').open('w') as f:
                writer = csv.writer(f)
                writer.writerow(['source_row', 'origin_utc', 'origin_beijing', 'reasons'])
                for i in np.flatnonzero(excluded):
                    writer.writerow([int(i), timestamp(original[i]), timestamp(original[i], True),
                                     '|'.join(k for k, v in reasons.items() if v[i])])
        with (out / f'provenance_{split}.csv').open('w') as f:
            writer = csv.writer(f)
            writer.writerow(['row', 'source_row', 'origin_utc', 'origin_beijing', 'issue_utc', 'issue_beijing',
                             'available_beijing', 'nwp_age_hours', 'target_end_beijing', 'last_weather_lead'])
            for i, t in enumerate(origin):
                it = int(times['issue'][i])
                writer.writerow([i, int(keep[i]), timestamp(t), timestamp(t, True), timestamp(it), timestamp(it, True),
                                 timestamp(it + PROTOCOL.latency, True), (int(t) - it) / 3600,
                                 timestamp(t + 21600, True), int((times['future'][i, -1] - it) // 3600)])
    keys = sorted(all_keys)
    lookup = {key: i for i, key in enumerate(keys)}
    for split, p in plans.items():
        for name in ['future', 'past']:
            p[name + '_frames'] = np.array([[lookup[(int(t), int((v - t) // 3600))] for v in vs]
                                           for t, vs in zip(p['issue'], p[name])], dtype=np.int64)
        np.savez(out / f'plan_{split}.npz', **p)
    atomic_json(out / 'SAMPLE_SELECTION.json', exclusions)
    save(out / 'frame_issue_unix.npy', np.array([k[0] for k in keys], dtype=np.int64))
    save(out / 'frame_lead_hours.npy', np.array([k[1] for k in keys], dtype=np.int16))
    return plans, keys, exclusions


def source_coverage(out, raw_root, keys):
    """Check exact issue/lead identities before allocating a large frame store."""
    paths = [source_path(raw_root, *key) for key in keys]
    missing = []
    with (out / 'required_source_files.csv').open('w') as f:
        writer = csv.writer(f)
        writer.writerow(['frame', 'issue_unix', 'issue_utc', 'lead_hours', 'path', 'exists'])
        for i, (key, path) in enumerate(zip(keys, paths)):
            exists = path.is_file()
            if not exists:
                missing.append(str(path))
            writer.writerow([i, key[0], timestamp(key[0]), key[1], str(path), int(exists)])
    coverage = dict(timestamp=now(), root=str(raw_root), root_accessible=raw_root.is_dir(),
        required_files=len(keys), missing_count=len(missing), missing=missing,
        minimum_lead=min(k[1] for k in keys), maximum_lead=max(k[1] for k in keys),
        issues=len(set(k[0] for k in keys)), global_shape=[1, 14, 721, 1440],
        complete=not missing)
    atomic_json(out / 'SOURCE_COVERAGE.json', coverage)
    return paths, coverage


def extract_raw(out, raw_root, keys, workers):
    paths, coverage = source_coverage(out, raw_root, keys)
    if not coverage['complete']:
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"{coverage['missing_count']} required same-issue Pangu files unavailable; see SOURCE_COVERAGE.json")
    lat = np.load(OLD / 'static/weather_latitude.npy')
    lon = np.load(OLD / 'static/weather_longitude.npy')
    rows = np.rint((90 - lat) / .25).astype(int)
    cols = np.rint(lon / .25).astype(int)
    assert np.array_equal(np.diff(rows), np.ones(len(rows) - 1))
    assert np.array_equal(np.diff(cols), np.ones(len(cols) - 1))
    ys = slice(int(rows[0]), int(rows[-1]) + 1)
    xs = slice(int(cols[0]), int(cols[-1]) + 1)
    shape = (len(keys), 14, len(rows), len(cols))
    raw_path = out / 'raw_frames.npy'
    done_path = out / 'raw_done.npy'
    if raw_path.exists() and done_path.exists():
        raw = np.lib.format.open_memmap(raw_path, mode='r+')
        done = np.lib.format.open_memmap(done_path, mode='r+')
        assert raw.shape == shape and done.shape == (len(keys),)
    else:
        raw = mmap_output(raw_path, shape)
        done = mmap_output(done_path, (len(keys),), '?')
        done[:] = False
        done.flush()
    coverage.update(global_rows=[ys.start, ys.stop], global_columns=[xs.start, xs.stop])
    atomic_json(out / 'SOURCE_COVERAGE.json', coverage)
    def read(i):
        path = paths[i]
        before = path.stat()
        data = np.load(path, mmap_mode='r', allow_pickle=False)
        if data.shape != (1, 14, 721, 1440) or data.dtype != np.dtype('float32'):
            raise ValueError(f'Unexpected archive format: {path}: {data.shape}/{data.dtype}')
        block = np.array(data[0, :, ys, xs], dtype=np.float32, copy=True)
        del data
        if not np.isfinite(block).all():
            raise ValueError(f'Nonfinite required crop: {path}')
        negative = [int((block[c] < 0).sum()) for c in [6, 7]]
        block[[6, 7]] = np.maximum(block[[6, 7]], 0)
        after = path.stat()
        assert (before.st_size, before.st_mtime_ns) == (after.st_size, after.st_mtime_ns)
        return i, block, [i, *keys[i], str(path), before.st_size, before.st_mtime_ns,
                          hashlib.sha256(block.tobytes()).hexdigest(), *negative]
    pending = np.flatnonzero(~done)
    receipt = out / 'source_crop_receipts.csv'
    with receipt.open('a') as f, ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers) as pool:
        writer = csv.writer(f)
        if f.tell() == 0:
            writer.writerow(['frame', 'issue_unix', 'lead_hours', 'path', 'size', 'mtime_ns', 'clean_crop_sha256', 'q1000_clipped', 'q925_clipped'])
        start = time.monotonic()
        for first in range(0, len(pending), 128):
            ids = pending[first:first + 128]
            for i, block, row in pool.map(read, ids):
                raw[i] = block
                writer.writerow(row)
            raw.flush()
            f.flush()
            os.fsync(f.fileno())
            done[ids] = True
            done.flush()
            atomic_json(out / 'STATUS.json', dict(timestamp=now(), state='extracting_same_issue_crops',
                done=int(done.sum()), total=len(keys), workers=workers, elapsed_seconds=time.monotonic() - start))
            if first % 1024 == 0:
                print(f'raw {int(done.sum())}/{len(keys)}', flush=True)
    assert done.all()
    return raw


def fit_normalization(out, raw, train):
    ids = np.unique(np.concatenate([train['future_frames'].ravel(), train['past_frames'].ravel()]))
    total = np.zeros(14, np.float64)
    square = np.zeros(14, np.float64)
    count = 0
    for start in range(0, len(ids), 128):
        x = np.asarray(raw[ids[start:start + 128]], dtype=np.float64)
        total += x.sum((0, 2, 3))
        square += np.square(x).sum((0, 2, 3))
        count += x.shape[0] * x.shape[2] * x.shape[3]
    mean = total / count
    std = np.sqrt(np.maximum(square / count - mean ** 2, 0))
    assert np.all(std > 1e-6)
    static = out / 'cache/static'
    static.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for path in (OLD / 'static').glob('*.npy'):
        if path.stem not in ['nwp_mean', 'nwp_std']:
            shutil.copy2(path, static / path.name)
    mean, std = mean.astype('f4'), std.astype('f4')
    save(static / 'nwp_mean.npy', mean)
    save(static / 'nwp_std.npy', std)
    save(out / 'normalization_frame_index.npy', ids)
    atomic_json(out / 'NORMALIZATION.json', dict(fitted_split='train', frame_count=len(ids),
        value_count_per_channel=count, method='unique train-referenced physical crops, six past plus eight weather nodes',
        mean=mean.tolist(), std=std.tolist(), validation_or_test_used=False))
    return mean, std


def bilinear(values, ids, weights):
    # [..., channel, latitude, longitude] -> [..., station, channel]
    flat = values.reshape(*values.shape[:-2], -1)
    v = flat[..., ids]
    return (v * weights).sum(-1).swapaxes(-1, -2)


def dynamics(raw, ids, weights, station_lat):
    lat = np.load(OLD / 'static/weather_latitude.npy')
    lon = np.load(OLD / 'static/weather_longitude.npy')
    dphi = np.deg2rad(float(lat[1] - lat[0])) * 6371000
    dlam = np.deg2rad(float(lon[1] - lon[0])) * 6371000
    w = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64)
    def point(field):
        return (field.reshape(*field.shape[:-2], -1)[..., ids] * weights).sum(-1)
    phi = np.deg2rad(station_lat)
    f = 2 * 7.2921159e-5 * np.sin(phi)
    ug = -point(np.gradient(w[:, :, 5], dphi, axis=-2, edge_order=2)) / f
    vg = point(np.gradient(w[:, :, 5], dlam, axis=-1, edge_order=2)) / np.cos(phi) / f
    dtdy = point(np.gradient(w[:, :, 9], dphi, axis=-2, edge_order=2))
    dtdx = point(np.gradient(w[:, :, 9], dlam, axis=-1, edge_order=2)) / np.cos(phi)
    p = bilinear(w, ids, weights)
    adv = -(p[..., 11] * dtdx + p[..., 13] * dtdy) * 3600
    dpdt = np.gradient(p[..., 0], 1., axis=1) / 100
    dvdt = np.gradient(np.hypot(p[..., 1], p[..., 2]), 1., axis=1)
    return np.stack([ug / 10, vg / 10, (p[..., 1] - ug) / 10, (p[..., 2] - vg) / 10,
                     adv, dpdt, dvdt], axis=-1).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3).astype('f4')


def prepare_split(out, split, p, raw, mean, std, ids, weights, station_lat):
    root = out / 'cache' / split
    physical = out / 'physical' / split
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    physical.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    n, c = len(p['origin']), len(p['base'])
    copy_names = ['station_history', 'station_future', 'cluster_history', 'cluster_future',
                  'future_capacity_warning', 'sample_index', 'origin_phase']
    for name in copy_names:
        old = np.load(OLD / split / f'{name}.npy', mmap_mode='r')
        dest = mmap_output(root / f'{name}.npy', (n, *old.shape[1:]), old.dtype)
        for start in range(0, n, 512):
            dest[start:start + 512] = old[p['keep'][start:start + 512]]
        dest.flush()
        del dest
    issue = p['issue'][p['cycle']]
    arrays = dict(origin_unix=p['origin'], source_row_index=p['keep'], weather_cycle_index=p['cycle'],
        origin_issue_unix=issue, origin_available_unix=issue + PROTOCOL.latency,
        nwp_age_minutes=(p['origin'] - issue) // 60,
        weather_time_offset_hours=((p['future'][p['cycle']] - p['origin'][:, None]) / 3600).astype('f4'),
        weather_valid_unix=p['future'], weather_issue_unix=np.broadcast_to(p['issue'][:, None], (c, 8)),
        weather_available_unix=np.broadcast_to(p['issue'][:, None] + PROTOCOL.latency, (c, 8)),
        cycle_sample_index=np.arange(c, dtype=np.int64), cycle_origin_hour_unix=p['base'])
    for name, array in arrays.items():
        save(root / f'{name}.npy', array)
    with h5py.File(H5, 'r') as f:
        time_axis = f['power/timestamp_unix'][:]
        index = np.searchsorted(time_axis, p['future'])
        assert np.all(index < len(time_axis)) and np.array_equal(time_axis[index], p['future'])
        station_power = f['power/station_mw'][:]
        cap = f['stations/capacity_mw'][:]
        warning = f['power/capacity_warning'][:]
        target = (station_power[index] / cap).transpose(0, 2, 1)
        assert np.isfinite(target).all()
        save(root / 'weather_station_target.npy', target.astype('f4'))
        save(root / 'weather_station_capacity_warning.npy', warning[index].transpose(0, 2, 1))
    future = mmap_output(root / 'weather.npy', (c, 8, *raw.shape[1:]))
    local = mmap_output(out / 'local_weather' / f'{split}.npy', (c, 56, 8, 14))
    past = mmap_output(physical / 'past_weather.npy', (c, 56, 6, 14))
    dyn = mmap_output(physical / 'dynamics.npy', (c, 56, 8, 7))
    for start in range(0, c, 48):
        end = min(start + 48, c)
        x = np.asarray(raw[p['future_frames'][start:end]])
        normalized = (x - mean[None, None, :, None, None]) / std[None, None, :, None, None]
        future[start:end] = normalized
        local[start:end] = bilinear(normalized, ids, weights).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)
        history = np.asarray(raw[p['past_frames'][start:end]])
        history = (history - mean[None, None, :, None, None]) / std[None, None, :, None, None]
        past[start:end] = bilinear(history, ids, weights).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)
        dyn[start:end] = dynamics(x, ids, weights, station_lat)
        if start % 480 == 0:
            atomic_json(out / 'STATUS.json', dict(timestamp=now(), state='building_cache_and_physics', split=split, done=end, total=c))
            print(split, end, '/', c, flush=True)
    for array in [future, local, past, dyn]:
        array.flush()
    del future, local, past, dyn
    save(physical / 'past_valid_unix.npy', p['past'])
    save(physical / 'past_issue_unix.npy', np.broadcast_to(p['issue'][:, None], (c, 6)))
    save(physical / 'future_issue_unix.npy', np.broadcast_to(p['issue'][:, None], (c, 8)))
    # A previous-cycle disagreement cache is deliberately absent, not stale data.
    atomic_json(physical / 'READY.json', dict(timestamp=now(), n_samples=n, n_cycles=c,
        protocol=PROTOCOL.name, all_nodes_from_same_issue=True,
        historical_nodes=6, future_nodes=8, reference_nwp_is_available=True,
        prior_cycle_disagreement_available=False))
    return dict(sample_count=n, cycle_count=c, weather_shape=[8, *raw.shape[1:]],
        station_history_shape=[56, 24], station_future_shape=[56, 24])


def validate(out, plans, raw, mean, std, ids, weights):
    from wind_forcing.data.dataset import ForecastDataset
    from wind_forcing.data.operational import interpolate_nodes
    result = {}
    for split, p in plans.items():
        ds = ForecastDataset(out / 'cache', split, preload_weather=False, verbose=False)
        assert len(ds) == len(p['origin'])
        age = ds.arrays['nwp_age_minutes']
        assert age.min() >= PROTOCOL.latency_hours * 60
        assert age.max() <= (PROTOCOL.latency_hours + PROTOCOL.period_hours) * 60 - 15
        issue = p['issue'][p['cycle']]
        assert np.all(issue + PROTOCOL.latency <= p['origin'])
        assert np.all(issue + PROTOCOL.latency + PROTOCOL.period > p['origin'])
        if split == 'test':
            assert not any(mask.any() for mask in november_exclusions(p['origin']).values())
        local = np.load(out / 'local_weather' / f'{split}.npy', mmap_mode='r')
        past = np.load(out / 'physical' / split / 'past_weather.npy', mmap_mode='r')
        dyn = np.load(out / 'physical' / split / 'dynamics.npy', mmap_mode='r')
        for array in [local, past, dyn, ds.arrays['station_history'], ds.arrays['station_future']]:
            for a in range(0, len(array), 128):
                assert np.isfinite(array[a:a + 128]).all()
        probe = np.unique([0, len(p['base']) // 2, len(p['base']) - 1])
        physical = np.asarray(raw[p['future_frames'][probe]])
        expected = (physical - mean[None, None, :, None, None]) / std[None, None, :, None, None]
        np.testing.assert_allclose(ds.weather.values[probe], expected, atol=1e-6, rtol=1e-6)
        point = bilinear(expected, ids, weights).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)
        np.testing.assert_allclose(local[probe], point, atol=1e-6, rtol=1e-6)
        chosen = np.unique(np.linspace(0, len(p['origin']) - 1, 100).astype(int))
        query = np.arange(1, 25) / 4
        values = interpolate_nodes(local[p['cycle'][chosen], 0], ds.arrays['weather_time_offset_hours'][chosen], query)
        assert np.isfinite(values).all()
        source_h = np.load(OLD / split / 'station_history.npy', mmap_mode='r')
        source_y = np.load(OLD / split / 'station_future.npy', mmap_mode='r')
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(ds.arrays['station_history'], source_h[p['keep']])
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(ds.arrays['station_future'], source_y[p['keep']])
        result[split] = dict(samples=len(ds), cycles=len(p['base']), min_age_hours=float(age.min() / 60),
            max_age_hours=float(age.max() / 60), no_future_issue=True, all_nodes_same_issue=True,
            original_power_exactly_preserved=True, all_values_finite=True, no_endpoint_extrapolation=True,
            first_origin_beijing=timestamp(p['origin'][0], True), last_origin_beijing=timestamp(p['origin'][-1], True))
    return result


def activate_bundle(out, config):
    manifest = json.loads((out / 'cache/manifest.json').read_text())
    identity = manifest['operational_protocol']
    if identity['protocol'] != PROTOCOL.name:
        raise ValueError('Completed bundle does not match requested protocol')
    fingerprint = manifest['preprocess_fingerprint']
    success = json.loads((out / 'SUCCESS.json').read_text())
    if success['preprocess_fingerprint'] != fingerprint:
        raise ValueError('Completion marker and cache fingerprint disagree')
    audit = json.loads((out / 'AUDIT.json').read_text())
    if not audit.get('passed') or audit['manifest_sha256'] != digest(out / 'cache/manifest.json'):
        raise ValueError('Bundle audit does not match the completed cache')
    independent = out / 'INDEPENDENT_AUDIT.json'
    if PROTOCOL == operational.DAILY:
        if not independent.is_file():
            raise ValueError('Run audit_operational_pangu.py --bundle before activating the daily protocol')
        independent_audit = json.loads(independent.read_text())
        if (not independent_audit.get('passed') or
                independent_audit.get('preprocess_fingerprint') != fingerprint):
            raise ValueError('Independent audit is absent or belongs to another dataset')
    link = Path(config.get('cache_dir', PROJECT / 'data/model_cache_operational_08_20_lag12h_v1'))
    if not link.exists():
        link.symlink_to(out / 'cache', target_is_directory=True)
    if link.resolve() != (out / 'cache').resolve():
        raise ValueError(f'Cache link points to a different dataset: {link}')
    atomic_json(PROJECT / 'data/ACTIVE_DATASET.json', dict(timestamp=now(), protocol=identity['protocol'],
        cache_dir=str(link), bundle_dir=str(out), preprocess_fingerprint=fingerprint,
        local_weather_dir=str(out / 'local_weather'), physics_dir=str(out / 'physical'),
        source_forecast_root=identity['root'], existing_frozen_training_runs_retargeted=False,
        require_new_training_and_normalization=True, audit=str(out / 'AUDIT.json'),
        independent_audit=str(independent) if independent.is_file() else None))


def main():
    global PROTOCOL, OLD, H5, REQUIRE_SAME_SAMPLES
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--config', type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--root', type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--output', type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--workers', type=int, default=4)
    parser.add_argument('--activate', action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--plan-only', action='store_true', help='Write sample/source requirements without reading weather arrays or activating data')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.plan_only and args.activate:
        parser.error('--plan-only cannot activate an unbuilt dataset')
    config = json.loads(args.config.read_text()) if args.config else {}
    PROTOCOL = operational.PROTOCOLS[config.get('protocol', operational.TWICE_DAILY.name)]
    args.root = args.root or Path(config.get('source_forecast_root', DEFAULT_ROOT))
    args.output = args.output or Path(config.get('bundle_dir', DEFAULT_OUT))
    OLD = Path(config.get('sample_cache', OLD)).resolve()
    H5 = Path(config.get('source_power_h5', H5)).resolve()
    REQUIRE_SAME_SAMPLES = config.get('require_same_samples', False)
    out = args.output.resolve()
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    lock = (out / 'builder.lock').open('a+')
    fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
    if (out / 'SUCCESS.json').exists():
        if args.activate:
            activate_bundle(out, config)
            print('Activated:', out)
        print('Already complete:', out)
        return
    identity = dict(protocol=PROTOCOL.name, root=str(args.root.resolve()),
        power_source=str(H5), old_cache=str(OLD), source_manifest_sha256=digest(OLD / 'manifest.json'),
        source_power_h5_size=H5.stat().st_size, source_power_h5_mtime_ns=H5.stat().st_mtime_ns,
        power_timezone='Asia/Shanghai', forecast_filename_timezone='UTC',
        issue_hours_utc=list(range(0, 24, PROTOCOL.period_hours)),
        issue_hours_beijing=[(x + 8) % 24 for x in range(0, 24, PROTOCOL.period_hours)],
        availability_delay_hours=PROTOCOL.latency_hours,
        max_required_hourly_forecast_lead=PROTOCOL.maximum_lead_hours, past_nodes=6, future_nodes=8,
        future_node_alignment='floor(origin UTC hour) + [0..7] hours',
        power_origin_and_targets='exactly preserve selected samples' if REQUIRE_SAME_SAMPLES else 'preserve old samples except explicit November and boundary exclusions',
        require_same_samples=REQUIRE_SAME_SAMPLES,
        regional_power_delay_minutes=config.get('regional_power_delay_minutes', 15),
        excluded_month='2024-11', exclusion='local history/target/NWP valid window; UTC or local November issue',
        forecast_power_steps=24, power_frequency_minutes=15,
        full_test_evaluation_performed=False, data_preparation_only=True,
        code_hashes={str(x.relative_to(PROJECT)): digest(x) for x in [Path(__file__), PROJECT / 'src/wind_forcing/data/operational.py', PHYSICS]})
    fingerprint = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(identity, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
    if (out / 'PROTOCOL.json').exists():
        assert json.loads((out / 'PROTOCOL.json').read_text())['preprocess_fingerprint'] == fingerprint, 'Cannot resume with altered source/protocol/code'
    atomic_json(out / 'PROTOCOL.json', dict(**identity, preprocess_fingerprint=fingerprint))
    atomic_json(out / 'STATUS.json', dict(timestamp=now(), state='planning', pid=os.getpid()))
    plans, keys, exclusions = build_plans(out, args.root)
    if args.plan_only:
        _, coverage = source_coverage(out, args.root, keys)
        atomic_json(out / 'STATUS.json', dict(timestamp=now(), state='planned' if coverage['complete'] else 'awaiting_source',
            required_files=len(keys), missing_files=coverage['missing_count'], sample_selection=exclusions,
            training_ready=False, training_started=False))
        print(json.dumps(dict(output=str(out), source_complete=coverage['complete'],
            required_files=len(keys), missing_files=coverage['missing_count'], samples=exclusions), ensure_ascii=False))
        return
    raw = extract_raw(out, args.root, keys, max(1, min(args.workers, 8)))
    mean, std = fit_normalization(out, raw, plans['train'])
    static = out / 'cache/static'
    lat = np.load(static / 'weather_latitude.npy')
    lon = np.load(static / 'weather_longitude.npy')
    slat = np.load(static / 'station_latitude.npy')
    slon = np.load(static / 'station_longitude.npy')
    ids, _, weights = build_station_patch_metadata(lat, lon, slat, slon, 'bilinear', 1)
    assert ids.shape == weights.shape == (56, 4)
    save(static / 'bilinear_patch_index.npy', ids)
    save(static / 'bilinear_weight.npy', weights)
    physics = load_physics()
    near, geometry, mask = physics.neighbor_geometry(slat, slon)
    for name, value in [('neighbor_ids', near), ('neighbor_geometry', geometry), ('neighbor_mask', mask)]:
        save(out / 'physical' / f'{name}.npy', value)
    dem = PROJECT / 'artifacts/column_weather_20260928_v1/data/station_dem_srtm30m.json'
    elevations = json.loads(dem.read_text())['records']
    save(out / 'physical/elevation.npy', np.array([x['terrain_elevation_m'] for x in elevations], dtype='f4'))
    summaries = {}
    for split, plan in plans.items():
        summaries[split] = prepare_split(out, split, plan, raw, mean, std, ids, weights, slat)
    old_manifest = json.loads((OLD / 'manifest.json').read_text())
    arrays = []
    for path in sorted((out / 'cache').glob('*/*.npy')):
        a = np.load(path, mmap_mode='r')
        arrays.append(dict(relative_path=str(path.relative_to(out / 'cache')), file=path.name, shape=list(a.shape), dtype=str(a.dtype)))
    manifest = dict(schema_version=1, preprocess_fingerprint=fingerprint, source_h5=str(H5),
        raw_forecast_root=str(args.root), operational_protocol=identity,
        weather_values_are_prestandardized=True, no_spatial_interpolation=True, no_temporal_interpolation=True,
        crop=old_manifest['crop'], station_count=56, total_capacity_mw=old_manifest['total_capacity_mw'],
        history_power_steps=24, forecast_power_steps=24, future_nwp_nodes=8,
        target_lead_hours=(np.arange(1, 25) / 4).tolist(), splits=summaries, arrays=arrays,
        local_weather_dir=str(out / 'local_weather'), physics_dir=str(out / 'physical'))
    atomic_json(out / 'cache/manifest.json', manifest)
    atomic_json(out / 'local_weather/manifest.json', dict(preprocess_fingerprint=fingerprint,
        source_cache=str(out / 'cache'), method='bilinear same-issue standardized Pangu', splits=summaries))
    audit = validate(out, plans, raw, mean, std, ids, weights)
    atomic_json(out / 'AUDIT.json', dict(timestamp=now(), passed=True, splits=audit,
        source_frames=len(keys), sample_selection=exclusions, normalization_train_only=True,
        test_data_prepared=True, test_model_evaluation_performed=False,
        old_scientific_runs_unchanged=True, manifest_sha256=digest(out / 'cache/manifest.json')))
    atomic_json(out / 'SUCCESS.json', dict(timestamp=now(), preprocess_fingerprint=fingerprint, audit_passed=True))
    atomic_json(out / 'STATUS.json', dict(timestamp=now(), state='complete', splits=summaries))
    if args.activate:
        activate_bundle(out, config)
    print(json.dumps(dict(output=str(out), audit=audit), ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    try:
        main()
    except BaseException as error:
        # Keep partial extraction resumable; never publish a ready dataset on failure.
        print(f'FAILED: {type(error).__name__}: {error}', file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        raise
```

- project/scripts/merge_verified_pangu_crops.py

SHA-256: 01c187096b58c193d1592ea4b345927e3bbdcfb4831c29f17571cb74689cd5bc

```
"""Reuse identical physical crops across protocols, never normalized caches or models."""
from pathlib import Path
import argparse
import csv
import fcntl
import hashlib
import json
import os
import sys
import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'src'))
from wind_forcing.data.operational import source_path
from prepare_operational_pangu import atomic_json, digest, now


def merge(old, out):
    lock = (out / 'builder.lock').open('a+')
    fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
    assert not (out / 'SUCCESS.json').exists(), 'Only merge into an unfinished raw store'
    protocol = json.loads((out / 'PROTOCOL.json').read_text())
    assert json.loads((old / 'AUDIT.json').read_text())['passed']
    assert (old / 'SUCCESS.json').is_file()
    for name in ['weather_latitude', 'weather_longitude', 'nwp_channel_name']:
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(np.load(old / f'cache/static/{name}.npy'),
                                      np.load(Path(protocol['old_cache']) / f'static/{name}.npy'))
    oi = np.load(old / 'frame_issue_unix.npy')
    ol = np.load(old / 'frame_lead_hours.npy')
    lookup = {(int(t), int(l)): i for i, (t, l) in enumerate(zip(oi, ol))}
    ni = np.load(out / 'frame_issue_unix.npy')
    nl = np.load(out / 'frame_lead_hours.npy')
    raw = np.load(out / 'raw_frames.npy', mmap_mode='r+')
    done = np.load(out / 'raw_done.npy', mmap_mode='r+')
    previous = np.load(old / 'raw_frames.npy', mmap_mode='r')
    assert raw.shape[1:] == previous.shape[1:] == (14, 24, 25)
    with (old / 'source_crop_receipts.csv').open() as f:
        receipts = {int(r['frame']): r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
    candidates = [int(i) for i in np.flatnonzero(~done) if (int(ni[i]), int(nl[i])) in lookup]
    # Fresh source values independently verify relocated-file identity across years/leads.
    fresh = set(np.asarray(candidates)[np.unique(np.linspace(0, len(candidates)-1, min(18, len(candidates))).astype(int))].tolist()) if candidates else set()
    merged, skipped, verified = [], [], []
    fields = ['frame','issue_unix','lead_hours','path','size','mtime_ns','clean_crop_sha256','q1000_clipped','q925_clipped']
    with (out / 'source_crop_receipts.csv').open('a') as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=fields)
        for start in range(0, len(candidates), 256):
            batch = []
            for i in candidates[start:start+256]:
                j = lookup[(int(ni[i]), int(nl[i]))]
                receipt = receipts[j]
                path = source_path(protocol['root'], int(ni[i]), int(nl[i]))
                st = path.stat()
                if (st.st_size, st.st_mtime_ns) != (int(receipt['size']), int(receipt['mtime_ns'])):
                    skipped.append(i)
                    continue
                block = np.asarray(previous[j])
                assert np.isfinite(block).all()
                assert hashlib.sha256(block.tobytes()).hexdigest() == receipt['clean_crop_sha256']
                if i in fresh:
                    global_data = np.load(path, mmap_mode='r')
                    assert global_data.shape == (1, 14, 721, 1440) and global_data.dtype == np.dtype('f4')
                    check = np.array(global_data[0, :, 171:195, 483:508])
                    check[[6, 7]] = np.maximum(check[[6, 7]], 0)
                    np.testing.assert_array_equal(block, check)
                    verified.append(i)
                raw[i] = block
                writer.writerow(dict(receipt, frame=i, path=str(path)))
                batch.append(i)
            raw.flush(); f.flush(); os.fsync(f.fileno())
            done[batch] = True; done.flush(); merged.extend(batch)
    report = dict(timestamp=now(),old_bundle=str(old),new_bundle=str(out),
                  preprocess_fingerprint=protocol['preprocess_fingerprint'],
                  source_receipts_sha256=digest(old/'source_crop_receipts.csv'),
                  reused=len(merged),skipped_source_metadata_mismatch=len(skipped),
                  fresh_source_comparisons=verified,all_reused_crop_hashes_verified=True,
                  normalized_features_or_statistics_reused=False,remaining=int((~done).sum()))
    np.save(out / 'merged_old_frame_indices.npy', np.asarray(merged, dtype='i8'))
    atomic_json(out / 'MERGE.json', report)
    print(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)
```

```
"""Independent timestamp, source-value and BASE/SEL loader checks; no model eval."""
from pathlib import Path
import argparse
import csv
import hashlib
import importlib.util
import json
import sys
from datetime import datetime
import h5py
import numpy as np
import torch

PROJECT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT / 'src'))
from wind_forcing.data.operational import plan_times, source_path, november_exclusions, unix, PROTOCOLS
from wind_forcing.data.operational_farm import OperationalFarmData


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--bundle', type=Path, default=Path('/shared_data_ssd3/liying/operational_pangu_20261027_v1'))
    args = parser.parse_args()
    torch.set_num_threads(1)
    bundle = args.bundle.resolve()
    assert json.loads((bundle / 'AUDIT.json').read_text())['passed']
    assert (bundle / 'SUCCESS.json').exists()
    cache = bundle / 'cache'
    protocol = json.loads((bundle / 'PROTOCOL.json').read_text())
    availability = PROTOCOLS[protocol['protocol']]
    manifest = json.loads((cache / 'manifest.json').read_text())
    assert manifest['preprocess_fingerprint'] == protocol['preprocess_fingerprint']
    for name, expected in protocol['code_hashes'].items():
        assert hashlib.sha256((PROJECT / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest() == expected
    frame_issue = np.load(bundle / 'frame_issue_unix.npy')
    frame_lead = np.load(bundle / 'frame_lead_hours.npy')
    raw = np.load(bundle / 'raw_frames.npy', mmap_mode='r')
    receipts = {}
    with (bundle / 'source_crop_receipts.csv').open() as f:
        for row in csv.DictReader(f):
            receipts[int(row['frame'])] = row
    assert len(receipts) == len(raw)
    for i in range(len(raw)):
        assert hashlib.sha256(np.asarray(raw[i]).tobytes()).hexdigest() == receipts[i]['clean_crop_sha256']
    checks = []
    # Compare fresh external crops at stratified positions with the extracted store.
    for i in np.unique(np.linspace(0, len(raw) - 1, 18).astype(int)):
        p = source_path(protocol['root'], int(frame_issue[i]), int(frame_lead[i]))
        data = np.load(p, mmap_mode='r')
        crop = np.array(data[0, :, 171:195, 483:508])
        crop[[6, 7]] = np.maximum(crop[[6, 7]], 0)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(raw[i], crop)
        checks.append(str(p))
    h5 = PROJECT / 'data/jilin_56farm_pad8.h5'
    with h5py.File(h5, 'r') as f:
        times = f['power/timestamp_unix'][:]
        power = f['power/station_mw'][:]
        capacity = f['stations/capacity_mw'][:]
        for split in ['train', 'val', 'test']:
            origin = np.load(cache / split / 'origin_unix.npy')
            p = plan_times(origin, availability)
            np.testing.assert_array_equal(np.load(cache / split / 'origin_issue_unix.npy'), p['issue'])
            np.testing.assert_array_equal(np.load(cache / split / 'origin_available_unix.npy'), p['available'])
            selected = np.unique(np.linspace(0, len(origin) - 1, 40).astype(int))
            positions = np.searchsorted(times, origin[selected])
            np.testing.assert_array_equal(times[positions], origin[selected])
            hpos = positions[:, None] + np.arange(-23, 1)
            ypos = positions[:, None] + np.arange(1, 25)
            np.testing.assert_array_equal(times[hpos], origin[selected, None] + np.arange(-23, 1)[None] * 900)
            np.testing.assert_array_equal(times[ypos], origin[selected, None] + np.arange(1, 25)[None] * 900)
            h = np.load(cache / split / 'station_history.npy', mmap_mode='r')
            y = np.load(cache / split / 'station_future.npy', mmap_mode='r')
            np.testing.assert_array_equal(h[selected], (power[hpos] / capacity).transpose(0, 2, 1))
            np.testing.assert_array_equal(y[selected], (power[ypos] / capacity).transpose(0, 2, 1))
            local_year = (origin + 8 * 3600).astype('datetime64[s]').astype('datetime64[Y]').astype(int) + 1970
            assert (local_year == {'train': 2022, 'val': 2023, 'test': 2024}[split]).all()
            if split == 'test':
                assert not any(x.any() for x in november_exclusions(origin, availability).values())
    # Exhaustive per-day switch invariants on 2023, including month/year rollovers.
    origins = unix('2023-01-01 00:00:00') + np.arange(365 * 96) * 900
    issue = plan_times(origins, availability)['issue']
    changes = np.flatnonzero(np.diff(issue)) + 1
    local_hours = ((origins[changes] + 28800) % 86400) / 3600
    expected_hours = {(x + availability.latency_hours + 8) % 24
                      for x in range(0, 24, availability.period_hours)}
    assert set(local_hours) == expected_hours
    assert len(changes) == 365 * (24 // availability.period_hours)
    loaders = []
    for features in ['BASE', 'SEL']:
        train = OperationalFarmData(cache, 'train', 0, features, limit=128)
        for split in ['val', 'test']:
            data = OperationalFarmData(cache, split, 0, features, stats=train.stats, limit=128)
            batch = data.batch(slice(0, 16))
            assert data.h.shape == data.y.shape == (128, 24)
            assert data.x.shape[-1] == (22 if features == 'SEL' else 0)
            assert all(torch.isfinite(x).all() for x in batch.values())
            assert np.all(data.available_unix <= data.origin)
            loaders.append(dict(feature_set=features, split=split, samples=data.n,
                                weather_shape=list(data.weather.shape), feature_shape=list(data.x.shape)))
        invalid = dict(train.stats, data_fingerprint='legacy')
        try:
            OperationalFarmData(cache, 'val', 0, features, stats=invalid, limit=4)
        except ValueError:
            pass
        else:
            raise AssertionError('Legacy statistics incorrectly accepted')
    result = dict(timestamp=datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(), passed=True,
        protocol=availability.name, preprocess_fingerprint=manifest['preprocess_fingerprint'],
        raw_crop_hashes_verified=len(raw), fresh_archive_frames_verified=checks,
        original_h5_power_time_and_values_verified=True, local_year_splits_verified=True,
        exhaustive_2023_switches_verified=len(changes), loader_checks=loaders,
        stale_normalization_rejected=True, test_model_evaluation_performed=False)
    (bundle / 'INDEPENDENT_AUDIT.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + '\n')
    print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

```
def sha(path):
    path=path.resolve()
    if path not in hash_cache:
        h=hashlib.sha256()
        with path.open('rb') as f:
            for chunk in iter(lambda:f.read(4*1024*1024),b''):h.update(chunk)
        hash_cache[path]=h.hexdigest()
    return hash_cache[path]
```

```
for k,m in enumerate(['mae','rmse']):
    values=[pairs[p][m]['change_percent'] for p in keypairs]
    ax.barh(y+(k-.5)*.32,values,height=.30,color=colors[k],label=m.upper())
    for j,v in enumerate(values):ax.text(v+(.018 if v>=0 else -.018),j+(k-.5)*.32,f'{v:+.3f}%',va='center',ha='left' if v>=0 else 'right',fontsize=9)
ax.set_yticks(y,labels);ax.invert_yaxis();ax.axvline(0,color='0.3',lw=.8);ax.set_xlim(-.9,1.0)
ax.set_xlabel('Relative error change vs matched Transformer (%) — negative is better')
ax.set_title('2023 selection-set results: 55 farms × 5 seeds');ax.legend(loc='lower right');ax.grid(axis='x',alpha=.15);ax.set_axisbelow(True)
fig.savefig(OUT/'matched_transformer_comparison.png',dpi=180);fig.savefig(OUT/'matched_transformer_comparison.svg');plt.close(fig)

def mdtable(headers,rows):
    return '|'+ '|'.join(headers)+'|\n|'+'|'.join(['---']*len(headers))+'|\n'+''.join('|'+ '|'.join(map(str,row))+'|\n' for row in rows)
def pairtable(ps):
    rows=[]
    for a,b in ps:
        c=pairs[a,b];rows.append([a,b,f"{c['mae']['change_percent']:+.3f}%",f"{c['rmse']['change_percent']:+.3f}%",f"{c['high_power_rmse']['change_percent']:+.3f}%",f"{c['mae']['farm_wins']}/55",f"{c['rmse']['farm_wins']}/55"])
    return mdtable(['候选','参照','ΔMAE','ΔRMSE','Δ高功率RMSE','MAE改善站数','RMSE改善站数'],rows)

seeds=read('per_seed_summary.csv');sp=read('per_seed_comparisons.csv');farms=read('per_farm_comparisons.csv')
detail='# 全55风场与逐种子明细\n\n所有百分比均为100×(候选/参照−1)，负值更好。逐站汇总使用全部5个seed；原始单元见 [全部模型逐风场逐seed指标](all_farm_seed_metrics.csv) 和 [逐配对单元](paired_farm_seed_comparisons.csv)。\n\n'
detail+='## 关键比较的每个随机种子\n\n'
for a,b in keypairs:
    detail+=f'### {a} 对 {b}\n\n'
    raw={(r['group'],r['seed']):r for r in seeds};rows=[]
    for seed in map(str,range(42,47)):
        x=raw[a,seed];z=raw[b,seed];c=next(r for r in sp if r['candidate']==a and r['reference']==b and r['seed']==seed)
        rows.append([seed,f"{float(x['mae']):.6f}",f"{float(z['mae']):.6f}",f"{float(c['mae_change_percent']):+.4f}%",f"{float(x['rmse']):.6f}",f"{float(z['rmse']):.6f}",f"{float(c['rmse_change_percent']):+.4f}%",c['mae_farms_won'],c['rmse_farms_won']])
    detail+=mdtable(['seed','候选MAE','参照MAE','ΔMAE','候选RMSE','参照RMSE','ΔRMSE','MAE胜站','RMSE胜站'],rows)+'\n'
detail+='## 全55站：三个核心比较\n\n不删去退化风场，不按结果挑评价站点；每格给出5seed等权指标变化与双指标同时改善的seed数。\n\n'
core=[('W0_R3','TF_R3'),('INDEPENDENT_W0_R3','INDEPENDENT_TF_R3'),('ENC_neutral_E2L','TF_R3')]
cohort=json.loads((P/'runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/assets/COHORT.json').read_text());rows=[]
for sid,name in zip(cohort['station_ids'],cohort['names']):
    row=[sid,name]
    for a,b in core:
        c=next(x for x in farms if x['candidate']==a and x['reference']==b and x['station_id']==sid)
        row.append(f"{float(c['mae_change_percent']):+.3f}% / {float(c['rmse_change_percent']):+.3f}% / {c['both_seed_wins']}/5")
    rows.append(row)
detail+=mdtable(['风场ID','名称','共享W0-R3：ΔMAE/ΔRMSE/双胜seed','独立W0-R3：ΔMAE/ΔRMSE/双胜seed','共享E2L：ΔMAE/ΔRMSE/双胜seed'],rows)
(OUT/'FARM_AND_SEED_DETAILS_CN.md').write_text(detail)

text='''# 每日起报新协议：完整重训结果与路线审查

> **用户最新约束：编码不作为机制贡献或独立贡献。** E2L/E2C只作为架构选择与消融；此前围绕简化编码组织贡献的建议已撤回。现有内部编码对照尚不能区分与TF比较中的编码和骨干效应，需要同编码跨骨干对照。见 [编码定义、现有对照和后续定位](ENCODING_ROLE_CLARIFICATION_20261115_CN.md)。

**2571/2571项已全部完成，失败0；最终完成时间2026-11-15 19:41（北京时间）。当前原版W0/E2C以及CBH路线不支持“0–6 h全面优于Transformer”。最有希望的本模型候选是去掉柱坐标与天气非线性映射的E2L共享版本，但仍未通过高功率退化约束，也尚无独立训练或新风场迁移验证。**

本文只使用新协议结果。若以现有结果选择已见风场的预测方案，2023选模后的TF全参数微调在核心部署方案中表现最好；这不构成本模型性能优势。若必须体现本模型优势，优先验证E2L，不应继续把CBH、复杂柱坐标编码或新风场迁移写成已得到支持的主线。

|补充调参与共享对照、消融191项＋逐站独立1100项|1291/1291|11-15 19:41|

共享run中的`FAIL_NO_LARGE_SCALE_PERSONALIZATION`是预定性能门槛未通过的标签，不是训练失败；`personalization_started:false`是该收集器自身未触发后续的历史字段。本轮微调已按用户授权由独立followup执行并完成，不得据此误判1100项尚未运行。

## 比较口径

- 任务0–6 h、24个15 min预测点；Pangu每天00 UTC，15 h后可用；邻站/区域功率可用至起点。2022训练，2023选超参数与检查点；固定55站，每站验证起点29647个。
- **W0**是本框架的无轨迹条件模型，默认天气编码E2C；**TF**是已调参Transformer。**BASE**使用本站历史、天气和站点信息；**R3**增加SEL物理/状态特征、近邻汇总与三环区域功率。单风场预测输出、参数共享训练、是否使用邻站输入是三个不同维度。
- E2L使用原始14天气通道与三层线性映射，保留W0其余结构及R3输入；E2N是同输入、同参数数量但含GELU的控制。E2L不是新风场迁移模型，也不是已经验证的独立单站模型。
- 指标是容量因子误差，不是MW。表中数值为55站×5seeds等权汇总，百分比为`100×(候选/参照−1)`；负数更好。改善站数比较每站完整5seed均值，不能替代逐seed原值。高功率按起点当前容量因子≥0.75定义，不是按未来目标筛选。
- [逐风场逐seed原始指标](analysis/daily_protocol_review_20261115/all_farm_seed_metrics.csv)、[关键逐seed与全55站明细](analysis/daily_protocol_review_20261115/FARM_AND_SEED_DETAILS_CN.md)、[所有配对原值](analysis/daily_protocol_review_20261115/paired_farm_seed_comparisons.csv)保留全部改善和退化。
- 2023同时用于选择和报告，尤其微调允许第0轮参与选模，相对底座的MAE收益存在选择优势。不能把这些差异直接当作独立泛化证据。描述性风场bootstrap没有处理空间依赖、多重比较或选模，不能据其宣称独立统计显著。2024尚未在本轮复评，且历史已看过，只能称探索性跨年复评。

## 主要比较

'''
text+=pairtable(keypairs)
text+='''
![匹配Transformer对照](analysis/daily_protocol_review_20261115/matched_transformer_comparison.png)

1. **原版W0共享R3没有全面赢。** MAE降低0.183%，RMSE增加0.227%，高功率RMSE增加0.619%；仅19/55站两项均改善。
2. **独立训练没有解决RMSE问题。** W0-R3相对独立TF-R3的MAE降低0.501%，40/55站改善、5个seed总体MAE都改善；但RMSE增加0.239%，只有18/55站改善，5个seed总体RMSE全部退化。275个站点×seed配对中，MAE胜185个，RMSE仅胜99个。仅本站BASE也有同样分歧，RMSE增加0.655%。
3. **微调不是W0优于TF的证据。** W0和TF都可从各自底座改善，但全参数微调后W0对TF的MAE/RMSE分别增加0.108%/0.369%。固定E1/E6比较也没有反转这点。
4. **E2L是当前最值得继续验证的本模型候选。** 相对同输入共享TF，MAE降低0.457%、RMSE降低0.216%，改善站数42/55和33/55；双指标同时改善28/55站。5个seed的MAE都好，RMSE只有3/5个好。高功率RMSE增加0.153%，超过原预定“退化≤0.1%”门槛，因此整体门槛仍未通过。
5. **E2L的比较边界很具体。** 它目前只在共享训练中有完整结果。与经过逐站选模的TF全参数微调作部署方案比较时，MAE/RMSE仍增加0.505%/0.484%；这不是同训练方式的结构归因比较，但说明它还不是当前最优部署方案。

主要方案的绝对指标如下，全部属于2023选择集：

'''
show=['SHARED_W0_BASE','TF_BASE','W0_R3','TF_R3','TIDE_R3','TFT_R3','M2_CBH','ENC_neutral_E2L','INDEPENDENT_W0_BASE','INDEPENDENT_TF_BASE','INDEPENDENT_W0_R3','INDEPENDENT_TF_R3','W0_P1_FULL','TF_P1_FULL']
text+=mdtable(['模型组','MAE','RMSE','高功率RMSE','参数量'],[[g,*[f"{float(summary[g][m]):.6f}" for m in ['mae','rmse','high_power_rmse']],summary[g]['parameter_count']] for g in show])

## 单风场训练方式

'''
text+=pairtable([('INDEPENDENT_W0_BASE','SHARED_W0_BASE'),('INDEPENDENT_W0_R3','W0_R3'),('INDEPENDENT_TF_BASE','TF_BASE'),('INDEPENDENT_TF_R3','TF_R3'),('W0_P1_FULL','W0_R3'),('TF_P1_FULL','TF_R3')])

在现有固定55个已见风场上，共享参数总体优于从随机初始化逐站独立训练。独立W0-R3对共享W0-R3的MAE/RMSE增加2.524%/1.748%，改善仅4/55和7/55站；TF也有相同方向。独立训练沿用对应共享调参配置，并未为每站独立搜索超参数，故不能概括为“独立训练必然差”。共享与独立的每站样本曝光轮数相同，总优化器更新数不同。

共享训练仍逐风场输出预测，符合单风场预测任务；R3需要业务上允许访问邻站/区域观测。若要求权重完全独立或只允许本站输入，就必须用对应独立/BASE结果，不能拿共享R3替代。

微调是对已在共享训练中见过的风场做个性化。W0全参数/输出层分别有104/275、118/275个单元最终选回第0轮，TF为69/275、67/275；因此选模后的改善不能与一个固定训练轮数方案直接等同。**没有目标风场留出实验，不能写新风场迁移优势。**

## 消融与机制

### 信息输入有效，但不是W0独有收益

'''
text+=pairtable([('W0_R3','SHARED_W0_BASE'),('TF_R3','TF_BASE'),('INDEPENDENT_W0_R3','INDEPENDENT_W0_BASE'),('INDEPENDENT_TF_R3','INDEPENDENT_TF_BASE')])
text+='''
共享W0的R3对BASE改善2.768%/2.272%，共享TF改善3.095%/2.663%；独立训练的两模型也都在55/55站双指标均改善。R3是有价值的信息扩展，不能据此把全部增益归因于W0结构。

从完整R3中移除各项的变化如下；正值表示移除后更差：

'''
ablations=[]
for f in ['W0','TF']:
    for suffix in ['LOCAL_SEL','NO_SEL','NO_NEAR','NO_RINGS','NO_DENSITY','NO_DYNAMICS','NO_STABILITY','NO_STATE']:
        c=pairs[f'INPUT_{f}_{suffix}',f+'_R3']
        ablations.append([f,suffix,f"{c['mae']['change_percent']:+.3f}%",f"{c['rmse']['change_percent']:+.3f}%"])
text+=mdtable(['模型','消融','ΔMAE','ΔRMSE'],ablations)
text+='''
LOCAL_SEL保留本站SEL并移除近邻/三环；NO_SEL移除全部22维SEL。三环区域统计、动力、运行状态有较清晰增益；近邻汇总边际增益较小。此处不能写成“逐邻站传播机制得到验证”：当前近邻输入是池化历史加几何键，非完整逐邻站独立序列。

### 更复杂的天气表示没有稳定胜出

E2L对原E2C的MAE/RMSE改善0.275%/0.442%；对同参数GELU控制E2N改善0.267%/0.456%，该对照5个seed双指标总体均改善。E2L对单层E0仅改善0.253%/0.228%，逐seed方向不一致，说明优化参数化等因素还需区分，不能直接归为物理规律。E3分组编码没有优于E2C。

E2L是仿射层的组合；它支持“当前任务中过强天气非线性未必有益”的经验假设，不支持原“柱坐标非线性编码是主要优势”的叙述。在E2L背景加入CBH后，汇总MAE/RMSE反而增加约0.206%/0.093%。

### 条件轨迹、区域记忆与滚动校正

'''
text+=pairtable([('M1_CB','W0_R3'),('M2_CBH','W0_R3'),('M2_CBH','M4H_WRONG_CBH'),('W0_RM','W0_R3'),('TF_RM','TF_R3'),('W0_P0_SHARED_Z1','W0_R3'),('TF_P0_SHARED_Z1','TF_R3')])
text+='''
CBH比CB好，不足以证明条件机制有效：它没有同时胜过无条件W0和错日期控制。RM两模型均未改善全时效双指标。Z1明显降低RMSE，却使MAE恶化约3.5%–4.0%，属于目标权衡，不是整体增益。在主指标仍为MAE时，不建议据此继续扩大LM；LM档案完成不等于LM模型完成。

### 历史与天气交互

移除天气及全部天气派生SEL后，W0/TF的MAE都恶化约19.1%、RMSE约17.4%–17.6%；把本站历史替换为重复当前功率后，MAE恶化1.250%/1.028%，RMSE恶化1.348%/0.914%。天气在此0–6 h业务协议下不可忽略。

差分交互 `(有真实历史时的天气收益)−(重复当前值时的天气收益)`：W0的MAE/RMSE为0.000336/0.001080，41/55与49/55站为正；TF为0.000255/0.000492，37/55与39/55站为正。当前功率锚点与区域观测仍保留，这是给定这些信息后的预测交互，不是因果识别，也不是W0独占现象。

## 时效与风场边界

'''
hh=read('horizon_comparisons.csv');rows=[]
for a,b in core:
    for x in hh:
        if (x['candidate'],x['reference'])==(a,b):rows.append([a,x['horizon'],f"{float(x['mae_change_percent']):+.3f}%",f"{float(x['rmse_change_percent']):+.3f}%",x['mae_farms_won']+'/55',x['rmse_farms_won']+'/55'])
text+=mdtable(['相对同方式TF的候选','时效','ΔMAE','ΔRMSE','MAE胜站','RMSE胜站'],rows)
text+='''
原W0独立R3的最明确优势在0–1 h：MAE/RMSE改善1.680%/0.722%，53/55与43/55站改善；3–6 h RMSE反而增加0.452%。不能把0–1 h优势推广为已固定的0–6 h任务整体优势。E2L共享版本的三个时段汇总MAE/RMSE均改善，因而比原版更值得验证，但仍有seed及风场退化。

完整独立R3配对中，仅6/55站在5个seed上双指标都改善；共享E2L对TF仅3/55站达到这一更严格条件。可把这些站作为解释性案例，但它们由成绩筛出，不能成为证明总体优势的唯一评价来源。现已完成全部55站，建议直接保留全站统计和全站明细；若论文版面只能展示少数站，应按容量、地域或训练期功率特性预定抽样，并同时提供全55站附件。

## 建议路线与贡献边界

**当前决策：原版E2C-CBH、新风场迁移、以及“独立训练全面压过TF”都不宜作为已确立的主路线。** 如果只考虑本轮已见风场的预测误差，TF全参数微调是核心部署方案中的优先参照；如果必须保持本模型，优先候选为“共享参数、逐风场输出、R3输入、E2L天气映射、无CB/CBH条件”。

建议把后续工作限定为有判别力的验证，避免先铺大矩阵：

1. 冻结E2L候选与同输入TF、E2C、E2N对照，先在预先固定的时间段/新增随机种子检查RMSE与高功率表现。现有E2L是在2023消融结果中挑出的优胜项，必须明确选择偏差。正式部署比较同时保留TF个性化参照。
2. 如果硬约束是完全独立训练，则补E2L逐站独立对照；当前1100项独立矩阵只有默认E2C的W0与TF，不能把共享E2L成绩直接挪用。若需新风场迁移，必须另做训练风场完全留出的设计，本站数据预算及适应步骤对W0/TF一致。
3. 保持0–6 h主任务和MAE/RMSE/高功率指标，冻结后再做2024探索性跨年复评。不能事后只保留有利时效、风场或将主指标改成RMSE来包装Z1。

以下仅作为研究工作的组织结构，不能预设为三个独立贡献；编码不列为机制或独立创新：

|方向|当前已有证据|尚不能宣称|
|---|---|---|
|整体单风场预测框架（方法贡献待验证）|完整框架已有性能与消融结果；编码只是实现选择|编码本身为创新；核心结构已在相同编码下胜过TF；独立/迁移已赢|
|消融归因（支撑证据）|输入删除、编码替换、错日期条件、历史×天气矩阵已重训|将编码替换单列为机制贡献；信息收益只属于W0；预测交互等于因果机制|
|业务适用边界：真实可用时刻、共享/独立/个性化、55站与分时效分析|统一每日00 UTC/15 h协议，全55站5seed原值，明确0–1 h与3–6 h差异|业务协议本身等于算法创新；只展示赢的风场可证明总体优势|

以上是方法、支撑分析、适用边界三部分；不把编码列为贡献，也不强凑三个已经成立的机制点。若后续E2L仍不能在约定条件下胜过同输入Transformer，应明确否决该优势叙事。

## 数据和代码入口

- 新天气缓存：[每日Pangu数据](/shared_data_ssd3/liying/operational_pangu_daily_20261114_v1/)，训练输入：[shared data](runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/data/)，固定风场：[COHORT](runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/assets/COHORT.json)。Pangu为预报，ERA5为再分析，不能称为场站实测天气。
- [共享模型实现](runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/code/model.py)、[补充天气编码/消融实现](runs/daily_ablation_restart_20261114_v1/code/model.py)、[单站与输入遮蔽数据实现](runs/daily_ablation_restart_20261114_v1/code/data.py)。
- [微调训练与第0轮选模](runs/daily_site_finetune_20261114_v1/code/train.py)、[Z1校正](runs/daily_site_finetune_20261114_v1/code/z1.py)。
- 三个冻结清单：[180](runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/manifest.json)、[1291](runs/daily_ablation_restart_20261114_v1/manifest.json)、[1100](runs/daily_site_finetune_20261114_v1/manifest.json)。
- [独立审计/配对分析脚本](scripts/review_daily_results_20261115.py)、[本文与明细生成脚本](scripts/render_daily_review_20261115.py)。先运行审计再运行渲染，任何缺项均不会生成完整配对结论。
- [机器可读比较与描述性区间](analysis/daily_protocol_review_20261115/COMPARISONS.json)、[比较汇总](analysis/daily_protocol_review_20261115/comparison_summary.csv)、[全部70组汇总](analysis/daily_protocol_review_20261115/group_summary.csv)、[每组5seed原值](analysis/daily_protocol_review_20261115/per_seed_summary.csv)、[分时效比较](analysis/daily_protocol_review_20261115/horizon_comparisons.csv)。
- 三个原始自动报告：[共享](runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/analysis/RESULTS_CN.md)、[消融/独立](runs/daily_ablation_restart_20261114_v1/analysis/RESULTS_CN.md)、[个性化](runs/daily_site_finetune_20261114_v1/analysis/RESULTS_CN.md)。这些报告中的自动门槛标签应结合本文的选模与配对边界解释。

本次已完成当前2571项重训的检查、补齐和分析；未启动额外科学实验。2024复评、条件性LM模型和目标风场留出迁移不属于已完成的2571项。
'''
(P/'DAILY_PROTOCOL_RESULTS_REVIEW_20261115_CN.md').write_text(text)
print('Rendered complete review, all-farm/seed appendix and comparison figure')
```
