## SOURCE: project/scripts/hourly_state_representation.py

- project/scripts/hourly_state_representation.py

SHA-256: 49e38e29df9d8490abfae45abbe640f94114009d30383ed5b276867bd5318808

```
"""Hourly supervision of the frozen v2 run; scientific gates stay in coordinator.py."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from collections import Counter
import datetime as dt
import fcntl
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shlex
import subprocess
import time

RUN_NAME = "daily_state_representation_20261116_v2"
PROJECTS = {
    "local4090": Path("/shared_data_ssd2/liying/wind_cluster_project_20261018/project"),
    "v1006001": Path("/home/liying/wind_physics_project/project"),
}


def now():
    return dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone(dt.timedelta(hours=8))).isoformat()


def read(path, default=None):
    return json.loads(path.read_text()) if path.exists() else default


def write(path, value):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temp = path.with_name(path.name + f".{os.getpid()}.tmp")
    temp.write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n")
    temp.replace(path)


def age(path):
    return round(time.time() - path.stat().st_mtime, 1) if path.exists() else None


def processes(run):
    """Match our own user and exact absolute script argument, never just a PID."""
    paths = {str(run / "operations" / (name + ".py")): name
             for name in ("scheduler", "coordinator")}
    paths.update({str(run / "code" / name): "workers"
                  for name in ("train.py", "reference_evaluation.py")})
    result = {name: [] for name in ("scheduler", "coordinator", "workers")}
    for proc in Path("/proc").glob("[0-9]*"):
        try:
            if proc.stat().st_uid != os.getuid():
                continue
            args = (proc / "cmdline").read_bytes().decode().split("\0")
            for path, name in paths.items():
                if path in args:
                    result[name].append(int(proc.name))
        except (OSError, UnicodeError):
            continue
    return result


def locked(path):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with path.open("a+") as handle:
        try:
            fcntl.flock(handle, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except BlockingIOError:
            return True
    return False


def verify_freeze(run):
    if not read(run / "operations/CHECKS.json", {}).get("passed"):
        raise RuntimeError("Startup checks have not passed")
    for name in ("SCIENCE_FREEZE.json", "OPERATIONS_FREEZE.json"):
        for rel, expected in read(run / name)["files"].items():
            digest = hashlib.sha256()
            with (run / rel).open("rb") as handle:
                for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(4 << 20), b""):
                    digest.update(chunk)
            if digest.hexdigest() != expected:
                raise RuntimeError(f"Frozen file changed: {rel}")


def inspect_host(run, host, python, recover=True):
    directory = run / "monitoring"
    directory.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    with (directory / "host.lock").open("a+") as lock:
        fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        status = read(run / "schedulers/status.json", {})
        assigned = read(run / "operations/ASSIGNMENTS.json", {})
        failures = sorted({*status.get("failed", []), *[
            p.parent.name for p in (run / "jobs").glob("*/FAILURE.json")
            if assigned.get(p.parent.name) == host]})
        stopped = (run / "operations/STOP.json").exists()
        complete = (run / "COMPLETE.json").exists()
        recent = read(directory / "restart_history.json", [])
        report = dict(host=host, timestamp=now(), stopped=stopped, complete=complete,
                      scheduler_age_seconds=age(run / "schedulers/status.json"),
                      failures=failures, actions=[], warnings=[])
        for name in (["coordinator", "scheduler"] if host == "local4090" else ["scheduler"]):
            found = processes(run)
            lockpath = run / ("operations/coordinator.lock" if name == "coordinator"
                              else "schedulers/scheduler.lock")
            held = locked(lockpath)
            if found[name] or held:
                if len(found[name]) > 1:
                    report["warnings"].append(f"Multiple {name} processes: {found[name]}")
                continue
            reason = None
            if complete or stopped or status.get("stopping"):
                reason = "completed_or_explicitly_stopped"
            elif name == "scheduler" and failures:
                reason = "failure_requires_inspection; circuit breaker preserved"
            elif name == "scheduler" and found["workers"]:
                # The frozen scheduler cannot adopt orphaned workers. Let them finish;
                # their inherited job locks remain authoritative until they exit.
                reason = "orphan_workers_still_running; waiting_for_safe_recovery"
            elif sum(x["component"] == name and time.time() - x["epoch"] < 21600
                     for x in recent) >= 2:
                reason = "restart_limit_2_per_6_hours"
            elif not recover:
                reason = "read_only_check"
            if reason:
                report["actions"].append(dict(component=name, action="not_restarted", reason=reason))
                continue
            try:
                verify_freeze(run)
                # Save the attempt before spawning, so failed launches are bounded too.
                record = dict(component=name, epoch=time.time(), timestamp=now())
                recent.append(record)
                write(directory / "restart_history.json", recent)
                env = dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS="1", MKL_NUM_THREADS="1",
                           OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS="1", PYTHONNOUSERSITE="1")
                args = [str(python), "-u", str(run / "operations" / (name + ".py"))]
                if name == "scheduler":
                    args += ["--host", host]
                with (run / "operations" / (name + ".log")).open("a") as log:
                    process = subprocess.Popen(args, cwd=run, env=env, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                                               stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, start_new_session=True)
                time.sleep(1)
                record.update(pid=process.pid, survived_initial_second=process.poll() is None)
                write(directory / "restart_history.json", recent)
                report["actions"].append(dict(action="restart_attempt", **record))
            except Exception as exc:
                report["warnings"].append(f"{name} recovery refused: {exc!r}")
        report["processes"] = processes(run)
        if not complete and not stopped:
            if report["scheduler_age_seconds"] is None or report["scheduler_age_seconds"] > 300:
                report["warnings"].append("Scheduler heartbeat missing or older than 5 minutes")
            if status.get("error"):
                report["warnings"].append(str(status["error"]))
            if failures:
                report["warnings"].append("Failed jobs are preserved for inspection")
        return report


def hourly():
    project = PROJECTS["local4090"]
    run = project / "runs" / RUN_NAME
    directory = run / "monitoring"
    directory.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    with (directory / "hourly.lock").open("a+") as lock:
        try:
            fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except BlockingIOError:
            print("Another hourly check is running; skipped.")
            return
        previous = read(run / "HOURLY_STATUS.json", {})
        report = dict(timestamp=now(), run=RUN_NAME, interval_seconds=3600, hosts={}, warnings=[])
        complete = read(run / "COMPLETE.json")
        report["complete"] = complete
        if complete is None:
            for host, base in PROJECTS.items():
                try:
                    if host == "local4090":
                        report["hosts"][host] = inspect_host(run, host, base / ".runtime/chronos/bin/python")
                    else:
                        command = "python3 - --host " + shlex.quote(host)
                        result = subprocess.run(
                            ["/usr/bin/ssh", "-o", "BatchMode=yes", "-o", "ConnectTimeout=8",
                             "-o", "ServerAliveInterval=10", "-o", "ServerAliveCountMax=2", host, command],
                            input=Path(__file__).read_text(), text=True, capture_output=True,
                            check=True, timeout=90)
                        report["hosts"][host] = json.loads(result.stdout)
                except Exception as exc:
                    report["warnings"].append(f"{host}: {exc!r}")
        status = read(run / "STATUS.json", {})
        gates = read(run / "analysis/GATES.json", {})
        report["progress"] = {k: status.get(k) for k in ["timestamp", "currently_enabled_training_jobs",
                              "completed_training", "diagnostic_evaluations_completed", "failures", "errors"]}
        report["active_by_host"] = dict(Counter(x["host"] for x in status.get("active", [])))
        report["gates"] = gates
        report["newly_enabled_stages"] = [k for k in ["stage2_enabled", "cross_backbone_enabled", "loss_enabled"]
                                           if gates.get(k) and not previous.get("gates", {}).get(k)]
        report["coordinator_age_seconds"] = age(run / "STATUS.json")
        if complete is None and (report["coordinator_age_seconds"] is None or report["coordinator_age_seconds"] > 300):
            report["warnings"].append("Coordinator heartbeat missing or older than 5 minutes")
        for host, state in report["hosts"].items():
            report["warnings"] += [f"{host}: {warning}" for warning in state["warnings"]]
            report["warnings"] += [f"{host}: {action['component']}: {action.get('reason', 'restart_failed')}"
                                   for action in state["actions"]
                                   if action["action"] == "not_restarted" and not state["stopped"]
                                   or action["action"] == "restart_attempt" and not action["survived_initial_second"]]
        report["warnings"] += [str(x) for x in status.get("errors", [])]
        # Do not call collect() here: the single locked coordinator owns every gate mutation.
        write(run / "HOURLY_STATUS.json", report)
        stamp = dt.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
        write(directory / (stamp + ".json"), report)
        print(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--host", choices=list(PROJECTS))
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.host:
        base = PROJECTS[args.host]
        print(json.dumps(inspect_host(base / "runs" / RUN_NAME, args.host,
                                      base / ".runtime/chronos/bin/python"), ensure_ascii=False))
    else:
        hourly()
```

## SOURCE: project/scripts/test_hourly_state_representation.py

- project/scripts/test_hourly_state_representation.py

SHA-256: b24b39d3492f811d7722252f00e54edb7f0eda106935f2c807e0bda705ae3657

```
"""Exercise recovery safeguards with temporary processes, never real training jobs."""
import hashlib
import fcntl
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
import unittest

from hourly_state_representation import inspect_host, processes, write


class RecoveryTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.run = Path(self.temp.name)
        for name in ["operations", "schedulers", "code", "jobs"]:
            (self.run / name).mkdir()
        for name, lock in [("scheduler", "schedulers/scheduler.lock"),
                           ("coordinator", "operations/coordinator.lock")]:
            (self.run / "operations" / (name + ".py")).write_text(
                "import fcntl,time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n"
                f"f=(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/{lock!r}).open('a+')\n"
                "fcntl.flock(f,fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB)\ntime.sleep(60)\n")
        (self.run / "code/train.py").write_text("import time\ntime.sleep(60)\n")
        write(self.run / "operations/CHECKS.json", {"passed": True})
        write(self.run / "operations/ASSIGNMENTS.json", {"failed_job": "local4090"})
        write(self.run / "SCIENCE_FREEZE.json", {"files": {}})
        write(self.run / "OPERATIONS_FREEZE.json", {"files": {
            str(p.relative_to(self.run)): hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            for p in (self.run / "operations").glob("*.py")}})

    def tearDown(self):
        for pids in processes(self.run).values():
            for pid in pids:
                try:
                    import os
                    os.kill(pid, 15)
                    os.waitpid(pid, 0)
                except (ProcessLookupError, ChildProcessError):
                    pass
        self.temp.cleanup()

    def check(self, recover=True):
        return inspect_host(self.run, "local4090", Path(sys.executable), recover)

    def test_missing_components_recover_once(self):
        report = self.check()
        self.assertEqual(len(report["actions"]), 2)
        self.assertTrue(all(x["survived_initial_second"] for x in report["actions"]))
        self.assertEqual(self.check()["actions"], [])
        self.assertEqual(processes(self.run), report["processes"])

    def test_stop_is_respected(self):
        write(self.run / "operations/STOP.json", {})
        self.assertTrue(all(x["reason"] == "completed_or_explicitly_stopped"
                            for x in self.check()["actions"]))
        self.assertFalse(any(processes(self.run).values()))

    def test_complete_is_respected(self):
        write(self.run / "COMPLETE.json", {"training_completed": 35})
        self.assertTrue(all(x["reason"] == "completed_or_explicitly_stopped"
                            for x in self.check()["actions"]))
        self.assertFalse(any(processes(self.run).values()))

    def test_held_scheduler_lock_prevents_duplicate_launch(self):
        with (self.run / "schedulers/scheduler.lock").open("a+") as lock:
            fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
            report = self.check(recover=False)
            self.assertFalse(any(x["component"] == "scheduler" for x in report["actions"]))

    def test_failure_circuit_is_not_bypassed(self):
        write(self.run / "jobs/failed_job/FAILURE.json", {})
        report = self.check(recover=False)
        scheduler = next(x for x in report["actions"] if x["component"] == "scheduler")
        self.assertIn("failure_requires_inspection", scheduler["reason"])

    def test_orphans_finish_before_scheduler_recovery(self):
        worker = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, str(self.run / "code/train.py")])
        try:
            time.sleep(.1)
            report = self.check(recover=False)
            scheduler = next(x for x in report["actions"] if x["component"] == "scheduler")
            self.assertIn("orphan_workers_still_running", scheduler["reason"])
            self.assertIsNone(worker.poll())
        finally:
            worker.terminate()
            worker.wait()

    def test_frozen_code_change_prevents_recovery(self):
        (self.run / "operations/scheduler.py").write_text("raise RuntimeError('changed')\n")
        report = self.check()
        self.assertEqual(len([x for x in report["warnings"] if "Frozen file changed" in x]), 2)
        self.assertFalse(any(processes(self.run).values()))

    def test_repeated_restarts_are_bounded(self):
        write(self.run / "monitoring/restart_history.json", [
            {"component": name, "epoch": time.time()} for name in ["scheduler", "coordinator"] for _ in range(2)])
        self.assertTrue(all(x["reason"] == "restart_limit_2_per_6_hours" for x in self.check()["actions"]))
        self.assertFalse(any(processes(self.run).values()))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/operations/collect.py

- project/runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/operations/collect.py

SHA-256: 061dbbffae304c8c01b1aa1bbac7c8e56118c5a06189d9ee886684402fedb9aa

```
from pathlib import Path
import sys,json,csv,os
RUN=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];sys.path.insert(0,str(RUN/'code'))
from settings import *
import numpy as np

def complete(spec):
    out=JOBS/spec['id']
    if not (out/'SUCCESS.json').exists():return None
    d=json.loads((out/'metrics.json').read_text());assert d['requested_spec']==spec and not d['smoke_only'];return d
def collect():
    specs=json.loads((RUN/'manifest.json').read_text());done={s['id']:d for s in specs if (d:=complete(s)) is not None}
    # Tuning: one configuration per family, equal-site MAE averaged across both seeds.
    for group in ['TF_BASE','TF_R3','W0_R3','TIDE_R3','TFT_R3']:
        jobs=[s for s in specs if s['stage']=='tune' and s['group']==group];out=ASSETS/'selected'/f'{group}.json'
        if not out.exists() and all(s['id'] in done for s in jobs):
            table=[]
            for ci in range(8):
                pair=[s for s in jobs if f'_c{ci}_' in s['id']];ms=[done[s['id']]['metrics'] for s in pair]
                table.append(dict(index=ci,config=pair[0]['config'],mae=float(np.mean([m['mae'] for m in ms])),rmse=float(np.mean([m['rmse'] for m in ms])),jobs=[s['id'] for s in pair]))
            best=min(table,key=lambda x:(x['mae'],x['index']));atomic_json(out,dict(timestamp=now(),group=group,config=best['config'],selected_by='2023 macroMAE averaged tune seeds40,41',selected_index=best['index'],table=table))
    # Assemble genuinely out-of-fold2022 references; final full model predicts2023.
    for seed in SEEDS:
        ids=[f'B0_r{seed}_f{f}' for f in [-1,0,1,2,3]];folder=DATA/f'reference_r{seed}'
        if not (folder/'READY.json').exists() and all(j in done for j in ids):
            folder.mkdir(exist_ok=True);shape=np.load(DATA/'train/h.npy',mmap_mode='r').shape
            path=folder/'train.partial.npy';a=np.lib.format.open_memmap(path,mode='w+',dtype='f4',shape=shape);covered=np.zeros(shape[0],bool)
            sources={}
            for f in range(4):
                jid=f'B0_r{seed}_f{f}';p=JOBS/jid/'reference_prediction.npy';assert sha(p)==done[jid]['reference_sha256'];held=np.load(ASSETS/f'fold{f}.npz')['heldout'];v=np.load(p,mmap_mode='r');assert v.shape==(len(held),*shape[1:]);a[held]=v;covered[held]=True;sources[jid]=done[jid]['checkpoint_sha256']
            assert covered.all() and np.isfinite(a).all();a.flush();del a;os.replace(path,folder/'train.npy')
            jid=ids[0];p=JOBS/jid/'reference_prediction.npy';assert sha(p)==done[jid]['reference_sha256'];v=np.load(p,mmap_mode='r');assert v.shape==np.load(DATA/'val/h.npy',mmap_mode='r').shape
            np.save(folder/'val.npy',v);sources[jid]=done[jid]['checkpoint_sha256']
            atomic_json(folder/'READY.json',dict(timestamp=now(),passed=True,seed=seed,sources=sources,train_mode='purged-quarter-OOF',val_mode='full2022_model',files={x:sha(folder/x) for x in ['train.npy','val.npy']}))
    grouped={}
    for s in specs:
        if s['id'] in done and s['stage'] in ['baseline','mechanism','regional_memory']:
            grouped.setdefault(s['group'],[]).append(done[s['id']])
    rows=[];byfarm=[]
    cohort=json.loads((ASSETS/'COHORT.json').read_text());nf=cohort['count']
    for group,ds in grouped.items():
        m=np.array([d['metrics']['per_farm_mae'] for d in ds]);r=np.array([d['metrics']['per_farm_rmse'] for d in ds])
        rows.append(dict(group=group,seeds=len(ds),mae=float(m.mean()),rmse=float(r.mean()),complete_5seeds=len(ds)==5))
        for s in range(nf):byfarm.append(dict(group=group,station_id=cohort['station_ids'][s],name=cohort['names'][s],seeds=len(ds),mae=float(m[:,s].mean()),rmse=float(r[:,s].mean())))
    for filename,data in [('shared_summary.csv',rows),('per_farm_metrics.csv',byfarm)]:
        if data:
            with (RUN/'analysis'/filename).open('w') as f:w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(data[0]));w.writeheader();w.writerows(data)
    def compare(a,b):
        aa=sorted(grouped[a],key=lambda d:d['spec']['seed']);bb=sorted(grouped[b],key=lambda d:d['spec']['seed']);res=dict(candidate=a,reference=b);rng=np.random.default_rng(1104)
        for key in ['mae','rmse']:
            x=np.array([d['metrics']['per_farm_'+key] for d in aa]);y=np.array([d['metrics']['per_farm_'+key] for d in bb]);xf=x.mean(0);yf=y.mean(0);delta=100*(xf.mean()/yf.mean()-1);wins=int((xf<yf).sum());draw=rng.integers(nf,size=(10000,nf));ci=np.quantile(100*(xf[draw].mean(1)/yf[draw].mean(1)-1),[.025,.975])
            res[key]=dict(change_percent=float(delta),farms_improved=wins,total_farms=nf,paired_farm_bootstrap95_percent=ci.tolist(),per_seed_change_percent=(100*(x.mean(1)/y.mean(1)-1)).tolist(),passed=bool(delta<=-.1 and wins>nf/2))
        hx=np.mean([d['metrics']['high_power_rmse'] for d in aa]);hy=np.mean([d['metrics']['high_power_rmse'] for d in bb]);res['high_power_rmse_change_percent']=float(100*(hx/hy-1));res['passed']=res['mae']['passed'] and res['rmse']['passed'] and res['high_power_rmse_change_percent']<=.1
        return res
    comparisons=[]
    pairs=[('W0_RM','W0_R3'),('TF_RM','TF_R3'),('W0_RM','TF_RM'),('W0_RM','TF_R3'),('W0_R3','TF_R3'),('M1_CB','W0_R3'),('M1_CB','M4_WRONG_CB'),('M2_CBH','W0_R3'),('M2_CBH','M1_CB'),('M2_CBH','M4H_WRONG_CBH'),('M3_ANCHOR','M1_CB'),('M5_TF_CB','TF_R3'),('M5_TF_CB','M5N_TF_NEUTRAL'),('M5_TF_CB','M5W_TF_WRONG_CB')]
    for a,b in pairs:
        if len(grouped.get(a,[]))==5 and len(grouped.get(b,[]))==5:comparisons.append(compare(a,b))
    gate=dict(timestamp=now(),stage='pending',complete_jobs=len(done),total_jobs=len(specs),comparisons=comparisons,personalization_started=False,test2024_read=False)
    needed=['TF_R3','TIDE_R3','TFT_R3','W0_R3','M1_CB','M2_CBH','M4_WRONG_CB','M4H_WRONG_CBH']
    if all(len(grouped.get(g,[]))==5 for g in needed):
        strongest=min(['TF_R3','TIDE_R3','TFT_R3'],key=lambda g:np.mean([x['metrics']['mae'] for x in grouped[g]]));passed=[]
        for cand,ctrl in [('M1_CB','M4_WRONG_CB'),('M2_CBH','M4H_WRONG_CBH')]:
            checks=[compare(cand,ref) for ref in ['W0_R3',ctrl,strongest]];comparisons.extend(checks)
            if all(c['passed'] for c in checks):passed.append(cand)
        gate.update(stage='PASS_READY_FOR_PERSONALIZATION' if passed else 'FAIL_NO_LARGE_SCALE_PERSONALIZATION',eligible=passed,strongest_same_input_baseline=strongest,selected_candidate=min(passed,key=lambda g:np.mean([x['metrics']['mae'] for x in grouped[g]])) if passed else None)
    atomic_json(RUN/'analysis/GATES.json',gate)
    status='# 共享训练阶段结果\n\n'+f'更新时间：{now()}。完成 {len(done)}/{len(specs)} 个任务；未读取2024。\n\n'
    if rows:
        status+='|组别|seeds|MAE|RMSE|\n|---|---:|---:|---:|\n'
        for r in sorted(rows,key=lambda r:r['mae']):status+=f'|{r["group"]}|{r["seeds"]}|{r["mae"]:.6f}|{r["rmse"]:.6f}|\n'
    status+='\n判定：'+gate['stage']+'。未完成5 seeds的组只展示进度，不作验收结论。\n'
    (RUN/'analysis/RESULTS_CN.md').write_text(status)
    return gate
if __name__=='__main__':print(json.dumps(collect(),ensure_ascii=False))
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/operations/pipeline.py

- project/runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/operations/pipeline.py

SHA-256: cea3f9324fb7454263b592daf4715c485e7e066d894b1a9293ceee6866c009c5

```
"""Resume weather preparation, audit the merged dataset, then deploy fresh training."""
from pathlib import Path
import fcntl
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import traceback

RUN=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PROJECT=RUN.parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0,str(RUN/'code'))
from settings import SOURCE,DATA,atomic_json,now,sha


def state(stage,**extra):
    atomic_json(RUN/'operations/PIPELINE_STATUS.json',dict(timestamp=now(),pid=os.getpid(),stage=stage,**extra))
    if not (RUN/'LAUNCH_STATUS.json').exists():
        prior=json.loads((RUN/'STATUS.json').read_text()) if (RUN/'STATUS.json').exists() else {}
        prior.update(timestamp=now(),state=stage,training_started=False,training_completed=0,**extra)
        if 'weather' in extra:
            prior.update(weather_raw_ready=extra['weather'].get('done'),weather_raw_total=extra['weather'].get('total'))
        prior['weather_data_rebuilt']=(SOURCE/'SUCCESS.json').exists()
        prior['training_pipeline_migration_complete']=(DATA/'READY.json').exists()
        atomic_json(RUN/'STATUS.json',prior)
    print(now(),stage,extra,flush=True)


def execute(label,script,*args):
    state(label)
    subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-u',str(script),*map(str,args)],cwd=PROJECT,check=True)


def weather():
    config=PROJECT/'configs/operational_pangu_d1_08_lag15h.json'
    while not (SOURCE/'SUCCESS.json').exists():
        with (SOURCE/'builder.lock').open('a+') as lock:
            try:
                fcntl.flock(lock,fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB)
                running=False
            except BlockingIOError:running=True
        if not running:
            execute('building_weather',PROJECT/'scripts/prepare_operational_pangu.py','--config',config,'--workers','8')
            break
        p=SOURCE/'STATUS.json'
        state('waiting_for_weather_builder',weather=json.loads(p.read_text()) if p.exists() else {})
        time.sleep(30)
    if not (SOURCE/'INDEPENDENT_AUDIT.json').exists():
        execute('independent_weather_audit',PROJECT/'scripts/audit_operational_pangu.py','--bundle',SOURCE)
    execute('activating_daily_dataset',PROJECT/'scripts/prepare_operational_pangu.py','--config',config,'--activate')


def freeze():
    paths=[*RUN.glob('code/**/*.py'),*RUN.glob('operations/*.py'),*RUN.glob('selection_inputs/*')]
    paths += [RUN/x for x in ['PROTOCOL.json','QC_RULES.json','manifest.json','DATA_FREEZE.json','EXECUTION_PLAN_CN.md','data/READY.json','operations/LOCAL_ADMISSION.json','operations/REMOTE_ADMISSION.json']]
    files={str(p.relative_to(RUN)):sha(p) for p in paths if p.is_file() and '__pycache__' not in p.parts}
    atomic_json(RUN/'SCIENCE_FREEZE.json',dict(timestamp=now(),files=files))


def run():
    lock=(RUN/'operations/pipeline.lock').open('a+');fcntl.flock(lock,fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB)
    weather()
    ready=DATA/'READY.json'
    if not ready.exists():execute('preparing_fixed_cohort_training_data',RUN/'code/prepare.py')
    assert json.loads(ready.read_text())['preprocess_fingerprint']==json.loads((SOURCE/'PROTOCOL.json').read_text())['preprocess_fingerprint']
    execute('generating_fresh_job_manifest',RUN/'code/plan.py')
    execute('checking_models_and_regional_time_alignment',RUN/'operations/checks.py')
    freeze()
    if not (RUN/'lm_archive/AUDIT.json').exists():
        with (RUN/'operations/prepare_lm.log').open('a') as log:
            archive=subprocess.Popen([sys.executable,'-u',str(RUN/'code/prepare_lm.py')],stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,start_new_session=True)
        atomic_json(RUN/'operations/LM_PID.json',dict(pid=archive.pid))
    if not (RUN/'LAUNCH_STATUS.json').exists():
        execute('deploying_two_v100_hosts_and_local_4090',RUN/'operations/launch.py')
    # Bootstrap verifies every transferred data hash and tests GPU arithmetic before training.
    state('waiting_for_all_host_bootstrap')
    from launch import remote,REMOTE
    while True:
        statuses={}
        for host in ['v1006001','v1006002']:
            code=f"from pathlib import Path;import json,time;p=Path({(REMOTE+'/operations/GPU_FIXTURE.json')!r});q=Path({(REMOTE+'/schedulers/status.json')!r});print(json.dumps(dict(fixture=p.exists(),scheduler=q.exists() and time.time()-q.stat().st_mtime<120)))"
            statuses[host]=json.loads(remote(host,code))
        local_status=RUN/'schedulers/status.json'
        statuses['local4090']=dict(fixture=(RUN/'operations/GPU_FIXTURE.json').exists(),scheduler=local_status.exists() and time.time()-local_status.stat().st_mtime<120)
        state('bootstrap_progress',hosts=statuses)
        if all(x['fixture'] and x['scheduler'] for x in statuses.values()):break
        time.sleep(30)
    log=(RUN/'operations/supervise.log').open('a')
    supervisor=subprocess.Popen([sys.executable,'-u',str(RUN/'operations/supervise.py')],stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,start_new_session=True)
    state('training_supervised',supervisor_pid=supervisor.pid,planned=180,old_weights_reused=False)
    atomic_json(RUN/'MIGRATION_READY.json',dict(timestamp=now(),passed=True,preprocess_fingerprint=json.loads(ready.read_text())['preprocess_fingerprint'],training_hosts=['v1006001','v1006002','local4090']))
    with (RUN/'operations/followup.log').open('a') as log:
        child=subprocess.Popen([sys.executable,'-u',str(RUN/'operations/followup.py')],stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,start_new_session=True)
    atomic_json(RUN/'operations/FOLLOWUP_PID.json',dict(pid=child.pid))


if __name__=='__main__':
    try:run()
    except Exception:
        state('failed',error=traceback.format_exc())
        raise
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_ablation_restart_20261114_v1/operations/collect.py

- project/runs/daily_ablation_restart_20261114_v1/operations/collect.py

SHA-256: fe451dd2fee0d66c76ef128eddbca81f9a9299d516373fafcbf441c9ecc27fa6

```
"""Complete matched tables; no historic metrics, partial groups never pass."""
from pathlib import Path
import sys,json,csv
RUN=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];sys.path.insert(0,str(RUN/'code'))
from settings import *
import numpy as np

def collect():
    # A source config is published only after all of its tune seeds finish.
    source=PROJECT/'runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1'
    for selected in (source/'assets/selected').glob('*.json'):
        dest=ASSETS/'selected'/selected.name
        if dest.exists():assert sha(dest)==sha(selected), 'Source selection changed'
        else:
            import shutil
            dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);tmp=dest.with_suffix('.copy.tmp');shutil.copy2(selected,tmp);os.replace(tmp,dest)

    specs=json.loads((RUN/'manifest.json').read_text());done={}
    for s in specs:
        out=JOBS/s['id']
        if not (out/'SUCCESS.json').exists():continue
        d=json.loads((out/'metrics.json').read_text());assert d['requested_spec']==s and not d['smoke_only'];done[s['id']]=d
    tune=[s for s in specs if s['stage']=='tune'];selected=ASSETS/'selected/W0_BASE.json'
    if not selected.exists() and all(s['id'] in done for s in tune):
        table=[]
        for ci in range(8):
            pair=[s for s in tune if f'_c{ci}_' in s['id']];assert len(pair)==2
            table.append(dict(index=ci,config=pair[0]['config'],mae=float(np.mean([done[s['id']]['metrics']['mae'] for s in pair])),jobs=[s['id'] for s in pair]))
        best=min(table,key=lambda x:(x['mae'],x['index']));atomic_json(selected,dict(timestamp=now(),group='W0_BASE',config=best['config'],selected_index=best['index'],table=table,selected_by='2023 macroMAE averaged seeds40,41'))
    nf=json.loads((ASSETS/'COHORT.json').read_text())['count'];cohort=json.loads((ASSETS/'COHORT.json').read_text());records=[]
    shared=PROJECT/'runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1'
    source_specs=json.loads((shared/'manifest.json').read_text())
    for s in source_specs:
        q=shared/'jobs'/s['id']
        if s['stage'] not in ['baseline','mechanism','regional_memory'] or not (q/'SUCCESS.json').exists():continue
        d=json.loads((q/'metrics.json').read_text());assert d['requested_spec']==s and s['preprocess_fingerprint']==specs[0]['preprocess_fingerprint'];records.append(d)
    records.extend(d for d in done.values() if d['spec']['stage']!='tune')
    groups={};rows=[]
    for d in records:
        s=d['spec'];m=d['metrics'];g=groups.setdefault(s['group'],{})
        sites=[s['station']] if 'station' in s else range(nf)
        for i,station in enumerate(sites):
            key=(s['seed'],station);assert key not in g
            row=dict(group=s['group'],seed=s['seed'],station=station,station_id=cohort['station_ids'][station],mae=m['per_farm_mae'][i],rmse=m['per_farm_rmse'][i],high_power_rmse=m['high_power_per_farm_rmse'][i],high_power_count=m['high_power_counts'][i]);g[key]=row;rows.append(row)
    complete={g:len(v)==nf*len(SEEDS) for g,v in groups.items()};summary=[];per_farm=[]
    for g,rs in groups.items():
        v=list(rs.values());summary.append(dict(group=g,cells=len(v),expected=nf*len(SEEDS),complete=complete[g],mae=float(np.mean([r['mae'] for r in v])),rmse=float(np.mean([r['rmse'] for r in v]))))
        for station in range(nf):
            vv=[x for x in v if x['station']==station]
            if vv:per_farm.append(dict(group=g,station_id=cohort['station_ids'][station],name=cohort['names'][station],seeds=len(vv),mae=float(np.mean([x['mae'] for x in vv])),rmse=float(np.mean([x['rmse'] for x in vv]))))
    comparisons=[]
    def compare(a,b):
        if not complete.get(a) or not complete.get(b):return
        keys=sorted(groups[a]);assert keys==sorted(groups[b]);res=dict(candidate=a,reference=b)
        for metric in ['mae','rmse']:
            x=np.array([groups[a][k][metric] for k in keys]).reshape(5,nf);y=np.array([groups[b][k][metric] for k in keys]).reshape(5,nf)
            res[metric]=dict(change_percent=float(100*(x.mean()/y.mean()-1)),farms_improved=int((x.mean(0)<y.mean(0)).sum()),per_seed_change_percent=(100*(x.mean(1)/y.mean(1)-1)).tolist())
        comparisons.append(res)
    for g in groups:
        if g.startswith(('INPUT_','FACTOR_')):compare(g,('_'.join(g.split('_')[1:2]))+'_R3')
        if g.startswith('ENC_'):compare(g,'W0_R3' if '_neutral_' in g else 'M2_CBH')
        if g.startswith('INDEPENDENT_'):
            base=g.removeprefix('INDEPENDENT_');compare(g,'SHARED_W0_BASE' if base=='W0_BASE' else base)
    for cond in ['neutral','cbh']:
        for a,b in [('E2N','E2L'),('E2N','E0'),('E2','E2N'),('E1','E0')]:compare(f'ENC_{cond}_{a}',f'ENC_{cond}_{b}')
    for inp in ['BASE','R3']:compare(f'INDEPENDENT_W0_{inp}',f'INDEPENDENT_TF_{inp}')
    compare('SHARED_W0_BASE','TF_BASE');compare('W0_R3','SHARED_W0_BASE')
    interaction=[]
    for f in ['W0','TF']:
        gs=[f+'_R3',f'FACTOR_{f}_H0W1',f'FACTOR_{f}_H1W0',f'FACTOR_{f}_H0W0']
        if all(complete.get(g) for g in gs):
            for metric in ['mae','rmse']:
                keys=sorted(groups[gs[0]]);a,b,c,d=[np.array([groups[g][k][metric] for k in keys]).reshape(5,nf) for g in gs]
                v=(c-a)-(d-b);interaction.append(dict(family=f,metric=metric,weather_gain_with_real_history=float((c-a).mean()),weather_gain_with_constant_history=float((d-b).mean()),interaction=float(v.mean()),positive_farms=int((v.mean(0)>0).sum()),per_seed=v.mean(1).tolist()))
    for name,values in [('all_seed_metrics.csv',rows),('summary.csv',summary),('per_farm_metrics.csv',per_farm)]:
        if values:
            with (RUN/'analysis'/name).open('w') as out:w=csv.DictWriter(out,fieldnames=list(values[0]));w.writeheader();w.writerows(values)
    result=dict(timestamp=now(),stage='COMPLETE_REVIEW_ABLATIONS' if len(done)==len(specs) else 'pending',completed=len(done),planned=len(specs),groups=summary,comparisons=comparisons,history_weather_interaction=interaction,test2024_read=False)
    atomic_json(RUN/'analysis/GATES.json',result)
    text='# 新协议消融与独立训练\n\n'+f'完成 {len(done)}/{len(specs)}。仅完整 55 站 × 5 seeds 的组进行比较；旧协议成绩未导入。\n\n|组别|完成单元|MAE|RMSE|\n|---|---:|---:|---:|\n'
    for s in summary:text+=f'|{s["group"]}|{s["cells"]}/{s["expected"]}|{s["mae"]:.6f}|{s["rmse"]:.6f}|\n'
    (RUN/'analysis/RESULTS_CN.md').write_text(text)
    return result
if __name__=='__main__':print(json.dumps(collect(),ensure_ascii=False))
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_site_finetune_20261114_v1/operations/collect.py

- project/runs/daily_site_finetune_20261114_v1/operations/collect.py

SHA-256: 770ded7dcd1210971df82d0f2eb2446d81843f5cc1351a9f4fd6a7a3728137d5

```
from pathlib import Path
import sys,json,csv,collections
RUN=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];sys.path.insert(0,str(RUN/'code'))
from settings import *
import numpy as np

def collect():
    manifest=json.loads((RUN/'manifest.json').read_text());cohort=json.loads((ASSETS/'COHORT.json').read_text());nf=cohort['count'];rows=[];done={};groups=collections.defaultdict(dict)
    for family in ['W0','TF']:
        for seed in SEEDS:
            d=json.loads((RUN/'pretrained'/f'{family}_r{seed}.metrics.json').read_text());m=d['metrics']
            for s in range(nf):
                row=dict(group=family+'_P0_SHARED',family=family,mode='P0_SHARED',seed=seed,station=s,station_id=cohort['station_ids'][s],name=cohort['names'][s],mae=m['per_farm_mae'][s],rmse=m['per_farm_rmse'][s],high_power_rmse=m['high_power_per_farm_rmse'][s],high_count=m['high_power_counts'][s],selected_epoch=0)
                rows.append(row);groups[row['group']][seed,s]=row
    for spec in manifest:
        out=JOBS/spec['id']
        if not (out/'SUCCESS.json').exists():continue
        d=json.loads((out/'metrics.json').read_text());assert d['requested_spec']==spec and not d['smoke_only'] and d['evaluation_year']==2023
        done[spec['id']]=d;m=d['metrics'];s=spec['station'];seed=spec['seed'];row=dict(group=spec['group'],family=spec['family'],mode=spec['mode'],seed=seed,station=s,station_id=spec['station_id'],name=cohort['names'][s],mae=m['mae'],rmse=m['rmse'],high_power_rmse=m['high_power_rmse'],high_count=m['high_power_counts'][0],selected_epoch=d['checkpoint_epoch'])
        rows.append(row);groups[row['group']][seed,s]=row
        for ep in [1,6]:
            path=out/f'epoch{ep}.json'
            if not path.exists():continue
            fixed=json.loads(path.read_text());assert sha(out/f'epoch{ep}.pt')==fixed['checkpoint_sha256']
            fm=fixed['metrics'];fr=dict(row,group=spec['group']+f'_E{ep}',mode=spec['mode']+f'_E{ep}',mae=fm['mae'],rmse=fm['rmse'],high_power_rmse=fm['high_power_rmse'],high_count=fm['high_power_counts'][0],selected_epoch=ep)
            rows.append(fr);groups[fr['group']][seed,s]=fr
        if spec['mode']=='P1_FULL':
            correction=json.loads((out/'Z1.json').read_text())
            assert correction['preprocess_fingerprint']==spec['preprocess_fingerprint']
            for label,mode in [('p0','P0_SHARED_Z1'),('e1','P1_FULL_E1_Z1')]:
                cm=correction['analyses'][label]['bin30'];cr=dict(row,group=spec['family']+'_'+mode,mode=mode,mae=cm['mae'],rmse=cm['rmse'],high_power_rmse=cm['high_power_rmse'],high_count=cm['high_power_count'],selected_epoch=0 if label=='p0' else 1)
                rows.append(cr);groups[cr['group']][seed,s]=cr
    summary=[]
    for group,rs in sorted(groups.items()):
        vals=list(rs.values());summary.append(dict(group=group,completed=len(vals),expected=nf*len(SEEDS),mae=float(np.mean([v['mae'] for v in vals])),rmse=float(np.mean([v['rmse'] for v in vals])),epoch0_selected=sum(v['selected_epoch']==0 for v in vals),complete=len(vals)==nf*len(SEEDS)))
    for name,data in [('per_station_seed.csv',rows),('summary.csv',summary)]:
        p=RUN/'analysis'/name;temp=p.with_suffix('.tmp')
        with temp.open('w') as f:w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(data[0]));w.writeheader();w.writerows(data)
        os.replace(temp,p)
    def compare(a,b):
        if len(groups[a])!=nf*len(SEEDS) or len(groups[b])!=nf*len(SEEDS):return None
        out=dict(candidate=a,reference=b);passed=True
        for metric in ['mae','rmse']:
            x=np.array([[groups[a][seed,s][metric] for s in range(nf)] for seed in SEEDS]);y=np.array([[groups[b][seed,s][metric] for s in range(nf)] for seed in SEEDS]);xf=x.mean(0);yf=y.mean(0);delta=100*(x.mean()/y.mean()-1);wins=int((xf<yf).sum());ok=bool(delta<=-.1 and wins>nf/2);passed&=ok
            rng=np.random.default_rng(1104);ix=rng.integers(nf,size=(10000,nf));ci=np.quantile(100*(xf[ix].mean(1)/yf[ix].mean(1)-1),[.025,.975])
            out[metric]=dict(change_percent=float(delta),farms_improved=wins,total_farms=nf,passed=ok,per_seed_change_percent=(100*(x.mean(1)/y.mean(1)-1)).tolist(),farm_bootstrap95_percent=ci.tolist())
        x=np.mean([v['high_power_rmse'] for v in groups[a].values() if v['high_count']>0]);y=np.mean([v['high_power_rmse'] for v in groups[b].values() if v['high_count']>0]);delta=float(100*(x/y-1));out['high_power_rmse_change_percent']=delta;out['passed']=bool(passed and delta<=.1);return out
    pairs=[(f'{f}_{m}',f'{f}_P0_SHARED') for f in ['W0','TF'] for m in ['P1_FULL','P2_HEAD']]+[(f'W0_{m}',f'TF_{m}') for m in ['P0_SHARED','P1_FULL','P2_HEAD']]+[(f'{f}_P1_FULL',f'{f}_P2_HEAD') for f in ['W0','TF']]
    pairs += [(f'W0_{m}_E{ep}',f'TF_{m}_E{ep}') for m in ['P1_FULL','P2_HEAD'] for ep in [1,6]]
    pairs += [(f'{f}_{m}_E{ep}',f'{f}_P0_SHARED') for f in ['W0','TF'] for m in ['P1_FULL','P2_HEAD'] for ep in [1,6]]
    pairs += [(f'{f}_{m}_Z1',f'{f}_{m}') for f in ['W0','TF'] for m in ['P0_SHARED','P1_FULL_E1']]
    pairs += [(f'W0_{m}_Z1',f'TF_{m}_Z1') for m in ['P0_SHARED','P1_FULL_E1']]
    comparisons=[c for a,b in pairs if (c:=compare(a,b)) is not None];complete=len(done)==len(manifest)
    selected={}
    if complete:
        for family in ['W0','TF']:
            opts=[s for s in summary if s['group'] in [family+'_'+m for m in ['P0_SHARED','P1_FULL','P2_HEAD']]];selected[family]=min(opts,key=lambda x:(x['mae'],x['group']))['group']
        comparisons.append(compare(selected['W0'],selected['TF']))
    gate=dict(timestamp=now(),stage='COMPLETE_REVIEW_PERSONALIZATION' if complete else 'pending',completed=len(done),planned=len(manifest),groups=summary,comparisons=comparisons,selected_by_2023_mae=selected,test2024_read=False,automatic_followup=False)
    atomic_json(RUN/'analysis/GATES.json',gate)
    text='# 共享预训练 + 逐站微调\n\n'+f'更新时间：{now()}。完成 {len(done)}/{len(manifest)} 次微调；55 站 × 5 seeds；2023 选择，未评价 2024。\n\n'
    text+='|组别|完成|MAE|RMSE|选第 0 轮|\n|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|\n'
    for s in summary:text+=f'|{s["group"]}|{s["completed"]}/275|{s["mae"]:.6f}|{s["rmse"]:.6f}|{s["epoch0_selected"]}|\n'
    text+='\n未满 275 项的组仅展示进度，不能与完整组比较。微调前第 0 轮也参加按 2023 MAE 的选模，选择集的改善不能当作独立确认。\n'
    for c in comparisons:text+=f'\n{c["candidate"]} 对 {c["reference"]}：MAE {c["mae"]["change_percent"]:+.4f}%（{c["mae"]["farms_improved"]}/55），RMSE {c["rmse"]["change_percent"]:+.4f}%（{c["rmse"]["farms_improved"]}/55），高功率 RMSE {c["high_power_rmse_change_percent"]:+.4f}%，验收 {c["passed"]}。\n'
    (RUN/'analysis/RESULTS_CN.md').write_text(text)
    return gate

if __name__=='__main__':print(json.dumps(collect(),ensure_ascii=False))
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/operations/collect.py

- project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/operations/collect.py

SHA-256: 799c47d65304d16b462013a4ec3891bdd605026004e6461207cbef63bfabbd1c

```
"""Predeclared gates. Missing jobs never count as evidence for a passing stage."""
from pathlib import Path
import sys,json,csv,collections
RUN=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];sys.path.insert(0,str(RUN/'code'))
import numpy as np
from settings import JOBS,ASSETS,atomic_json,now,sha


def records():
    groups=collections.defaultdict(dict)
    for group in ['R0','TF_R3']:
        for seed in range(42,47):
            groups[group][seed]=json.loads((RUN/'reference_metrics'/group/str(seed)/'metrics.json').read_text())
    for spec in json.loads((RUN/'manifest.json').read_text()):
        out=JOBS/spec['id']
        if not (out/'SUCCESS.json').exists():continue
        value=json.loads((out/'metrics.json').read_text())
        assert value['requested_spec']==spec and not value['smoke_only']
        groups[spec['group']][spec['seed']]=value
    return dict(groups)


def compare(groups,a,b):
    assert set(groups[a])==set(groups[b])==set(range(42,47))
    out=dict(candidate=a,reference=b)
    seed_improvements=[]
    for metric in ['mae','rmse','high_power_rmse']:
        key={'mae':'per_farm_mae','rmse':'per_farm_rmse','high_power_rmse':'high_power_per_farm_rmse'}[metric]
        x=np.array([groups[a][seed]['metrics'][key] for seed in range(42,47)])
        y=np.array([groups[b][seed]['metrics'][key] for seed in range(42,47)])
        assert x.shape==y.shape==(5,55) and np.isfinite(x).all() and np.isfinite(y).all()
        out[metric+'_change_percent']=float(100*(x.mean()/y.mean()-1))
        out[metric+'_farm_wins']=int((x.mean(0)<y.mean(0)).sum())
        out[metric+'_per_seed_change_percent']=(100*(x.mean(1)/y.mean(1)-1)).tolist()
        if metric!='high_power_rmse':seed_improvements.append(x.mean(1)<y.mean(1))
    out['both_metrics_seed_wins']=int(np.logical_and(*seed_improvements).sum())
    out['dual_improvement']=out['mae_change_percent']<0 and out['rmse_change_percent']<0
    out['main_pass']=bool(out['mae_change_percent']<=-.1 and out['rmse_change_percent']<=-.1 and
        out['mae_farm_wins']>=28 and out['rmse_farm_wins']>=28 and out['both_metrics_seed_wins']>=4 and
        out['high_power_rmse_change_percent']<=.1)
    return out


def enable(name,payload):
    path=RUN/'operations'/name
    if not path.exists():atomic_json(path,dict(timestamp=now(),**payload))


def collect():
    groups=records();complete={g for g,s in groups.items() if set(s)==set(range(42,47))}
    state=dict(timestamp=now(),complete_groups=sorted(complete),stage1_complete=False,
               stage2_enabled=False,cross_backbone_enabled=False,loss_enabled=False,finished=False)
    rows=[]
    for group in sorted(complete):
        for reference in ['R0','TF_R3','TF_E2L']:
            if reference in complete and group!=reference:rows.append(compare(groups,group,reference))
    def dual(a,b):
        row=compare(groups,a,b);rows.append(row);return row['dual_improvement']
    def main_pass(group):
        return all(compare(groups,group,ref)['main_pass'] for ref in ['R0','TF_R3','TF_E2L'] if group!=ref)
    first={'R0+H','R1','R2','R3','TF_E2L'}
    if first<=complete:
        state['stage1_complete']=True
        state['r3_mechanism']=all([dual('R3','R2'),dual('R3','R0+H')])
        state['r3_main_pass']=main_pass('R3')
        state['stage2_enabled']=compare(groups,'R3','R2')['dual_improvement']
        if state['stage2_enabled']:enable('ENABLE_STAGE2.json',dict(reason='R3 improves both metrics versus R2'))
        second={'R4','R4-noS','R4-wrongS'}
        stage2_done=not state['stage2_enabled'] or second<=complete
        state['stage2_complete_or_skipped']=stage2_done
        if state['stage2_enabled'] and second<=complete:
            state['r4_mechanism']=all([dual('R4',g) for g in ['R3','R4-noS','R4-wrongS']])
            state['r4_main_pass']=main_pass('R4')
            state['cross_backbone_enabled']=state['r4_mechanism'] and state['r4_main_pass']
            if state['cross_backbone_enabled']:enable('ENABLE_TF_R4.json',dict(reason='R4 passes mechanism and main gates'))
        if stage2_done:
            candidate='R4' if state.get('r4_mechanism') else 'R3' if state['r3_mechanism'] else 'R1' if main_pass('R1') else 'R0'
            path=ASSETS/'selected_candidate.json'
            value=dict(representation=candidate,rule=json.loads((RUN/'PROTOCOL.json').read_text())['loss_candidate_rule'])
            if path.exists():assert json.loads(path.read_text())==value,'Candidate selection is immutable'
            else:atomic_json(path,value)
            enable('ENABLE_LOSS.json',dict(candidate=candidate,selection_sha256=sha(path)))
            state.update(loss_enabled=True,loss_candidate=candidate)
            if {'LOSS_CANDIDATE','LOSS_TF'}<=complete:
                versus_tf=compare(groups,'LOSS_CANDIDATE','LOSS_TF');rows.append(versus_tf)
                versus_self=compare(groups,'LOSS_CANDIDATE',candidate);rows.append(versus_self)
                state['adopt_mixed_loss']=bool(versus_tf['dual_improvement'] and versus_self['mae_change_percent']<=0 and versus_self['rmse_change_percent']<=0)
                state['finished']=not state['cross_backbone_enabled'] or 'TF_R4' in complete
                state['method_qualified']=main_pass(candidate) if candidate!='R0' else False
    out=RUN/'analysis';out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    atomic_json(out/'GATES.json',state);atomic_json(out/'COMPARISONS.json',rows)
    summaries=[]
    for group in sorted(complete):
        values=[groups[group][s] for s in range(42,47)]
        summaries.append(dict(group=group,seeds=5,**{k:float(np.mean([v['metrics'][k] for v in values])) for k in ['mae','rmse','high_power_rmse']},
                              selected_epochs=[v['checkpoint_epoch'] for v in values]))
    atomic_json(out/'GROUPS.json',summaries)
    flat=[{k:v for k,v in r.items() if not isinstance(v,list)} for r in rows]
    if flat:
        with (out/'comparisons.csv').open('w') as f:
            w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(flat[0]));w.writeheader();w.writerows(flat)
    text=['# V2 实验进度与判定','',f'更新时间：{state["timestamp"]}。完整组：'+', '.join(sorted(complete))+'。',
          '', '仅完整的五种子组参与比较；2023 同时用于选择，结论不是独立测试结果。',
          '', '| 组 | MAE | RMSE | 高功率 RMSE |','|---|---:|---:|---:|']
    text += [f'| {r["group"]} | {r["mae"]:.8f} | {r["rmse"]:.8f} | {r["high_power_rmse"]:.8f} |' for r in summaries]
    text += ['', '详细阈值与阶段状态见 [GATES.json](GATES.json)，配对结果见 [comparisons.csv](comparisons.csv)。',
             '每项任务的 metrics.json 另含整点/非整点、大变化跟上比例、高功率上行/下行及生成式离散度。']
    (out/'README_CN.md').write_text('\n'.join(text)+'\n')
    return state

if __name__=='__main__':print(json.dumps(collect(),ensure_ascii=False))
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/README_CN.md

- project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/README_CN.md

SHA-256: d26401c0d8140a15631435b133d0281a15ebb79416a3e95c911b6b29c49b971b

```
# 状态条件中间表示 v2：执行入口

本轮落实 [v2 方案](../../STATE_CONDITIONED_REPRESENTATION_PLAN_20261115_CN.md)。进度与自动判定见 [analysis/README_CN.md](analysis/README_CN.md) 和 [STATUS.json](STATUS.json)。不能把尚未完成的条件阶段当作已有结果。

已配置服务器每小时整点巡检，按冻结门槛自动接续后续阶段；配置、恢复保护及检查记录入口见 [每小时巡检说明](HOURLY_MONITOR_CN.md)。

## 固定协议与任务

复用每日 00 UTC 起报、延迟 15 h、气象裁剪 9–45 h 的已核验缓存。55 站共享训练，2022 训练、2023 选轮，种子 42–46。有效 batch 512，AdamW、常数学习率 0.0002、weight decay 0.0001、骨干 dropout 0.05，最多 80 epoch、patience 10。每轮验证一次，严格最小 macro-MAE 选轮，全部指标使用同一权重。R0 与 TF_R3 的十项原训练不重跑。

- [manifest.json](manifest.json)：25 项阶段一；条件启动的 15 项 R4、5 项 TF+R4；10 项损失复核，最多 55 项训练。
- [diagnostic_manifest.json](diagnostic_manifest.json)：另有 10 项原权重推理，补齐 R0/TF 的诊断，不属于新增训练。
- [PROTOCOL.json](PROTOCOL.json)：启动前固定的超参数、损失、门槛和阶段规则。
- [SCIENCE_FREEZE.json](SCIENCE_FREEZE.json)、[DATA_FREEZE.json](DATA_FREEZE.json)：源码、配置、参照及数据哈希。
- [OPERATIONS_FREEZE.json](OPERATIONS_FREEZE.json)：结果产生前固定的判定、诊断与调度代码；[初始化修正记录](INITIALIZATION_REPAIR.json)说明正式结果产生前的一次 RNG 修正及重启。

## 代码与实现细节

[data.py](code/data.py) 继承原 [base_data.py](code/base_data.py)，保持原样本与常规输入，增加八个小时节点、时间偏移和 17 维 c。c 包含当前功率、1−当前功率、近 1 h 变化率、整个历史窗变化率、7 维原 SEL-STATE、三环当前值及 1 h 变化。24 个历史点实际覆盖 5.75 h，未增加第 25 个点。SEL 与区域特征沿用 2022 归一化。

[model.py](code/model.py) 只改天气查询分量；R0+H 单独扩大输出头并将新增输入权重置零。原 [backbone.py](code/backbone.py) 保留。E2L 是仿射复合，所以 R1 在实数运算下与 R0 等价；该组是数值/实现对照，不是独立机制。等价测试采用非零预测头，排除零头导致所有模型都输出持久性的伪等价。

[representation.py](code/representation.py) 的窗口生成器宽 32、1 层、2 heads、dropout 0；读取 z、相邻差分、节点相对时间及可选 c，输出 7 段 × 4 系数。固定高斯核宽 0.75 h、中心为小时段中点，展开为 24×4 后投影到天气潜空间。四维是可学习残差通道，不宣称每一维已有可辨识的物理含义。

TF_E2L 的 14 维天气走相同的三层无激活线性编码，4 维时间特征保持独立线性投影，两者相加；历史编码和输出头不变。

## R4 固定实现

教师只在训练读取 y−h 和潜空间插值相对起点的变化，不进入推理。教师采用高斯后验，先验标准差 0.1，KL 权重 0.001；流匹配目标停止对教师反传。学生用 8 步 Euler 积分，训练 4 条功率样本的均值、评价 8 条样本的均值。评价噪声按起点、风场、种子、样本编号固定，不依赖 batch，不消耗训练 RNG。中位数只报告，不参与选择。

目标为教师 MAE + 学生均值 MAE + 0.1 流匹配 MSE + 0.001 KL；损失复核对两个点预测项同时加入 0.2 MSE。R4 初始残差投影为零，初始输出等于 R1；参数样本有离散度，功率样本的离散度在投影学习后才出现。均值 MAE **不能保证不坍缩**，因此报告样本标准差和非零比例，并检查采样链路梯度。

R4-wrongS 只替换新生成器的 c，骨干原历史、SEL 和区域输入不变。供体限定为 2022 训练池、同月同钟点、至少相隔两天；训练与验证均使用固定供体。R4-noS 同样只移除新生成器的 c。

## 调度、判定与结果

[调度器](operations/scheduler.py) 使用本机 4090 的 0–3 卡及 v1006001 的 0–7 卡。任务充足时每卡两个进程；第三进程须通过耗时/总吞吐检查。短队列集中到较少卡，保留显存和主机内存余量。OOM 可减小 microbatch 并从稳定检查点恢复，有效 batch 仍是 512。[协调器](operations/coordinator.py) 回传远端结果并核验配置、权重哈希。

[判定器](operations/collect.py) 只比较完整五种子组。主门槛分别对 R0、TF_R3、TF_E2L 检查，避免“较强参照”的歧义。R3 对 R2 双指标改善才启动 R4 三组；R4 同时通过主门槛和三个机制对照后才启动 TF+R4。

损失复核候选按预写定顺序选择：机制通过的 R4；否则机制通过的 R3；否则通过主门槛的 R1；否则 R0。最后一种只复核已有模型的损失，不代表新机制成立。混合损失采用条件仍是方案中的双指标条件。

[diagnostics.py](code/diagnostics.py) 报告整点/非整点、真实变化超过 0.3 的 MSE 与跟上比例、高功率上行/下行、生成式离散度。诊断按 pooled cells 汇总，区别于选轮的风场等权指标，也区别于旧诊断的五种子集成后指标。

启动前检查在 `operations/CHECKS.json`，完整小样本训练在 `smoke_jobs/`；冒烟结果不是正式结果。公开仓库不含数据、权重和全部运行日志。

扩大到 10 seeds、统一轮数个性化和 2024 探索性复评依赖本轮结果与最终冻结，不提前读取 2024 标签或声称已完成。R0 从七种编码中按 2023 挑选，选择偏差仍在；10 seeds 只能检查随机种子稳定性，不能单独消除选择偏差。
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/HOURLY_MONITOR_CN.md

- project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/HOURLY_MONITOR_CN.md

SHA-256: 9117c5428cb77798d6abeca9c17488f7ae913d535b4282100d06bf662c4c0f9f

````
# 每小时检查与自动接续

本机 `liying` 用户的 cron 每小时整点执行一次 [巡检脚本](../../scripts/hourly_state_representation.py)，时区为北京时间（Asia/Shanghai）。cron 服务由服务器管理，退出当前对话或 SSH 不影响检查。最近一次结果见 [HOURLY_STATUS.json](HOURLY_STATUS.json)；逐次记录位于服务器的 `monitoring/YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS.json`，标准输出保存在 `monitoring/cron.log`。这些是服务器检查记录，不会自动发送聊天消息。

检查覆盖本机 4090 和 `v1006001` 的协调器、调度器、任务完成数、失败任务、心跳和阶段判定。现有 [coordinator.py](operations/coordinator.py) 每约 30 秒回收结果并按冻结规则判定，因此符合条件后无需等待下个整点。

- 25 项阶段一完成、R3 相对 R2 的 MAE 和 RMSE 均改善后，启动 15 项 R4/R4-noS/R4-wrongS。
- R4 同时通过主门槛和机制对照后，启动 5 项 TF_R4。
- 阶段二完成或按规则跳过后，按冻结顺序选择损失复核候选，启动候选与 TF 的 10 项混合损失训练。
- 全部应做任务及诊断结束后生成 [COMPLETE.json](COMPLETE.json)。之后巡检仅记录完成状态，不再恢复进程或启动训练。

巡检不直接写阶段启用文件，阶段决策仍由唯一持锁协调器执行。两台机器继续使用原多进程资源策略和固定任务分配。

恢复保护：核对精确进程路径与用户，并使用互斥锁；缺失的管理进程只有在冻结文件哈希和启动检查通过后才可恢复。尊重 STOP、完成标志和失败熔断，每组件六小时内最多恢复两次。如果调度器退出但其训练子进程仍存活，先让已有训练完成，下次巡检再判断恢复；不会重复开进程挤占资源。失败任务、心跳陈旧或网络中断记录在 `warnings`，不会清除失败标志或降低实验门槛。保护策略的临时进程验证见 [test_hourly_state_representation.py](../../scripts/test_hourly_state_representation.py)。

巡检脚本独立于冻结训练代码。10 seeds、个性化和 2024 复评仍需本轮结论与最终候选冻结，不在本次自动接续范围内。

服务器查看方式：

```bash
crontab -l
python3 -m json.tool /shared_data_ssd2/liying/wind_cluster_project_20261018/project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/HOURLY_STATUS.json
```
````

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/operations/checks.py

- project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/operations/checks.py

SHA-256: bb0f56b6bc0f146847034ebdbd88c09f1dc832e7ffe041b1774142bf4cdf1715

```
"""Actual-data checks for equivalence, label isolation, gradients and donor validity."""
from pathlib import Path
import argparse, copy, json, sys, time
RUN=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];sys.path.insert(0,str(RUN/'code'))
import numpy as np
import torch
from settings import atomic_json,now,sha,DATA,ASSETS
from data import SharedData
from backbone import SharedModel as Original
from model import SharedModel,configure
from train import resolve
from representation import lerp,stateless_noise


def main(device):
    configure();started=time.monotonic()
    specs={s['group']:resolve(s) for s in json.loads((RUN/'manifest.json').read_text()) if s['seed']==42 and s['representation']!='SELECTED'}
    data=SharedData('train',specs['R3']);ids=np.arange(16)*55+np.arange(16)
    b,y,_,_=data.batch(ids,device)
    assert b['hourly'].shape==(16,8,14) and b['state_c'].shape==(16,17)
    # Every quarter-hour phase, including the edge 6h prediction, must match.
    assert set(b['phase'].cpu().tolist())=={0,1,2,3}
    torch.testing.assert_close(lerp(b['hourly'],b['offset']),b['weather'],rtol=2e-6,atol=2e-6)
    base=Original(dict(specs['R1'],representation='R0')).to(device).eval()
    cpu_rng=torch.get_rng_state().clone()
    cuda_rng=torch.cuda.get_rng_state().clone() if device.startswith('cuda') else None
    for group in ['R0+H','R1','R2','R3','TF_E2L','R4']:
        original=Original(specs[group])
        expected_cpu=torch.get_rng_state().clone()
        expected_cuda=torch.cuda.get_rng_state().clone() if device.startswith('cuda') else None
        checked=SharedModel(specs[group])
        assert torch.equal(expected_cpu,torch.get_rng_state()),('CPU RNG changed',group)
        if expected_cuda is not None:assert torch.equal(expected_cuda,torch.cuda.get_rng_state()),('CUDA RNG changed',group)
    torch.set_rng_state(cpu_rng)
    if cuda_rng is not None:torch.cuda.set_rng_state(cuda_rng)
    torch.manual_seed(123)
    with torch.no_grad():
        # A zero final forecast head would make any equivalence test vacuous.
        base.head[-1].weight.normal_(std=.1);base.head[-1].bias.fill_(.03)
        expected=base(b)
    diffs={};gradients={}
    for group in ['R0+H','R1','R2','R3']:
        model=SharedModel(specs[group]).to(device).eval()
        model.head[-1].load_state_dict(base.head[-1].state_dict())
        out=model(b)
        torch.testing.assert_close(out,expected,rtol=2e-5,atol=2e-6)
        diffs[group]=float((out-expected).abs().max().detach())
        if group in ['R2','R3']:
            (out-y).square().mean().backward()
            gradient=float(model.window.output.weight.grad.abs().sum())
            assert gradient>0;gradients[group]=gradient
        if group=='R0+H':
            (out-y).square().mean().backward();assert model.head[0].weight.grad[:,-2:].abs().sum()>0
    # State isolation: changing c changes only R3's generator after a nonzero update.
    for group,uses_state in [('R2',False),('R3',True)]:
        model=SharedModel(specs[group]).to(device).eval()
        with torch.no_grad():model.window.output.weight.normal_(std=.1)
        z,_=model.latent(b);a=model.window(z,b['offset'],b['state_c']);other=model.window(z,b['offset'],b['state_c']+1)
        assert bool((a-other).abs().max()>1e-7)==uses_state
    for group in ['R4','R4-noS','R4-wrongS','TF_R4','TF_E2L']:
        model=SharedModel(specs[group]).to(device)
        head=model.head if model.family=='W0' else model.original.head
        last=[m for m in head.modules() if isinstance(m,torch.nn.Linear)][-1]
        with torch.no_grad():last.weight.normal_(std=.1)
        small={k:v[:4] for k,v in b.items()};target=y[:4]
        model.train();loss=model.training_loss(small,target);assert torch.isfinite(loss)
        loss.backward();assert all(p.grad is None or torch.isfinite(p.grad).all() for p in model.parameters())
        if model.generative:
            assert model.residual_projection.weight.grad.abs().sum()>0
            assert model.flow.output.weight.grad.abs().sum()>0
            # Verify power/flow gradients beyond the zero-initialized projection.
            optimizer=torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(),lr=.0002)
            optimizer.step();optimizer.zero_grad();model.training_loss(small,target).backward()
            assert model.teacher.output.weight.grad.abs().sum()>0
            assert model.flow.output.weight.grad.abs().sum()>0
            model.eval()
            def forbidden(*args,**kwargs):raise AssertionError('Teacher used at inference')
            handle=model.teacher.register_forward_pre_hook(forbidden)
            rng=torch.get_rng_state().clone()
            with torch.no_grad():
                draws=model.prediction_samples(small);mean=model(small)
                assert draws.shape==(4,8,24) and torch.isfinite(draws).all()
                assert float(draws.std(1).mean())>0
                torch.testing.assert_close(mean,draws.mean(1))
                parts=torch.cat([model({k:v[:2] for k,v in small.items()}),model({k:v[2:] for k,v in small.items()})])
                torch.testing.assert_close(parts,mean,rtol=2e-5,atol=2e-6)
                assert torch.equal(rng,torch.get_rng_state())
            handle.remove()
        gradients[group]=float(loss.detach())
    # A wrong-state donor comes from training only, same month/clock, >=2 days away.
    for split in ['train','val']:
        wrong=SharedData(split,specs['R4-wrongS'])
        origin=np.asarray(wrong.a['origin'])+28800
        train_origin=np.load(DATA/'train/origin.npy')[wrong.state_donor]+28800
        assert np.all(origin%86400==train_origin%86400)
        assert np.all(origin.astype('datetime64[s]').astype('datetime64[M]').astype(int)%12==train_origin.astype('datetime64[s]').astype('datetime64[M]').astype(int)%12)
        assert np.all(abs(origin//86400-train_origin//86400)>=2)
        wb,_,_,_=wrong.batch(ids[:4],device)
        assert not torch.allclose(wb['state_c'],b['state_c'][:4])
    frozen=json.loads((RUN/'DATA_FREEZE.json').read_text())
    for path,value in frozen['files'].items():
        assert sha(RUN/path)==value,('data changed',path)
    report=dict(timestamp=now(),passed=True,device=device,seconds=time.monotonic()-started,
                equivalence_max_errors=diffs,gradient_checks=gradients,all_four_phases=True,
                inference_has_no_teacher=True,batch_invariant_sampling=True,wrong_state_donors_valid=True,
                backbone_cpu_cuda_initialization_rng_preserved=True,
                data_files_hashed=len(frozen['files']))
    atomic_json(RUN/'operations/CHECKS.json',report);print(json.dumps(report),flush=True)

if __name__=='__main__':
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--device',default='cuda:0');main(p.parse_args().device)
```

```
"""Collect remote evidence and activate only predeclared, gate-qualified stages."""
from pathlib import Path

def remote(code):
    p=subprocess.run(['ssh','-o','BatchMode=yes','-o','ConnectTimeout=8',HOST,'python3 -c '+shlex.quote(code)],
                     check=True,capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=40)
    return json.loads(p.stdout)


def main():
    lock=(RUN/'operations/coordinator.lock').open('a+');fcntl.flock(lock,fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB)

                assert value['requested_spec']==by_id[jid] and not value['smoke_only'] and value['evaluation_year']==2023
                if value.get('no_training'):assert sha(RUN/by_id[jid]['checkpoint'])==value['checkpoint_sha256']
                else:assert sha(out/'best.pt')==value['checkpoint_sha256']
                atomic_json(out/'SUCCESS.json',dict(timestamp=now(),passed=True,source_host=HOST,verified_return=True))
            gates=collect()
            for p in [ASSETS/'selected_candidate.json',*sorted((RUN/'operations').glob('ENABLE_*.json'))]:
                if p.exists():
                    rel=str(p.parent.relative_to(RUN))
                    subprocess.run(['rsync','-a',str(p),HOST+':'+REMOTE+'/'+rel+'/'],check=True,timeout=30,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        except Exception as exc:

        localfile=RUN/'schedulers/status.json'

        active=(local.get('active',[]) if local_fresh else [])+(state.get('status',{}).get('active',[]) if state.get('fresh') else [])
        active=[x for x in active if not (JOBS/x['id']/'SUCCESS.json').exists()]
        progress={}
        for item in active:
            path=JOBS/item['id']/'progress.json'
            if path.exists():progress[item['id']]=json.loads(path.read_text())
        required=[s for s in specs if all((RUN/d).exists() for d in s['dependencies'])]
        finished=[s['id'] for s in required if (JOBS/s['id']/'SUCCESS.json').exists()]
        diagnostic_done=sum((JOBS/s['id']/'SUCCESS.json').exists() for s in diagnostic)
        failures=[s['id'] for s in specs+diagnostic if (JOBS/s['id']/'FAILURE.json').exists()]

        report_diagnostics()
        atomic_json(RUN.parents[1]/'ACTIVE_EXPERIMENT.json',dict(timestamp=now(),run=str(RUN),status_path=str(RUN/'STATUS.json'),
                    scope='v2 state representation; conditional stages; local4090 and v1006001'))
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/operations/efficiency_v3.py

- project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/operations/efficiency_v3.py

SHA-256: 8ddca8eb4543bbb373ba8a415f2262df22b7408b889d73c8ef34a0040d8ecca6

```
"""Robust paired update-cost windows and bounded, reversible concurrency trials."""
import json,time,datetime,statistics
from pathlib import Path

class EfficiencyGovernor:
    def __init__(self,jobs,ceiling=8):
        self.jobs=Path(jobs);self.ceiling=ceiling;self.cards={}
    def control(self,gpu,items,decision):
        now=time.time();members=tuple(sorted((j,it['p'].pid) for j,it in items));count=len(items)
        s=self.cards.setdefault(gpu,dict(members=(),count=0,changed=now,cap=self.ceiling,until=0,windows={},baseline={},reference={},bad=0,fingerprint=None,trial_from=0))
        if members!=s['members']:
            previous=s['count'];reference={j:statistics.median(v[-5:]) for j,v in s['windows'].items() if len(v)>=3}
            s.update(members=members,count=count,changed=now,windows={},reference=reference if count>previous else {},trial_from=previous if count>previous else 0,bad=0,fingerprint=None)
        if now>s['until'] and s['cap']<self.ceiling:s['cap']+=1;s['until']=now+180
        paired=[];absolute=[];fingerprint=[];ready=True
        for jid,it in items:
            try:r=json.loads((self.jobs/jid/'progress.json').read_text())
            except (OSError,ValueError):ready=False;continue
            stamp=datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(r['timestamp']).timestamp()
            if r['pid']!=it['p'].pid or stamp<s['changed']+10 or now-stamp>120:ready=False;continue
            key=(jid,it['p'].pid,r['epoch'],r['completed_steps']);fingerprint.append(key)
            w=s['windows'].setdefault(jid,[]);seen=s.setdefault('seen',{})
            if seen.get(jid)!=key and r.get('chunk_steps',25)>=10:
                w.append(r['seconds_per_step']);del w[:-5];seen[jid]=key
            if len(w)<3:ready=False;continue
            cost=statistics.median(w);prior=s['baseline'].get(jid)
            if prior is None or count<prior['count']:s['baseline'][jid]=dict(cost=cost,count=count)
            elif count==prior['count']:prior['cost']=.8*prior['cost']+.2*cost
            prior=s['baseline'][jid]
            if count>prior['count']:absolute.append(cost/prior['cost'])
            if jid in s['reference']:paired.append(cost/s['reference'][jid])
        ratio=statistics.median(absolute) if absolute else None
        marginal=statistics.median(paired) if paired else None
        gain=count/s['trial_from']/marginal if marginal and s['trial_from'] else None
        bad=ready and ((ratio is not None and ratio>1.5) or (marginal is not None and marginal>1.35) or (gain is not None and gain<1.02))
        if tuple(fingerprint)!=s['fingerprint']:
            s['bad']=s['bad']+1 if bad else 0;s['fingerprint']=tuple(fingerprint)
        decision['efficiency']=dict(version=4,cap=s['cap'],workers=count,slowdown_ratio=ratio,paired_incremental_slowdown=marginal,estimated_aggregate_gain=gain,max_slowdown=1.5,max_incremental_slowdown=1.35,waiting_for_step_calibration=not ready,stable_seconds=round(now-s['changed'],1),per_job_step_seconds={j:statistics.median(v) for j,v in s['windows'].items() if len(v)>=3})
        if decision.get('yield_count'):return decision
        if count>1 and s['bad']>=3:
            s.update(cap=count-1,until=now+180,bad=0)
            return dict(decision,launch=False,yield_count=1,reason='sustained_paired_throughput_regression')
        if count>=s['cap']:return dict(decision,launch=False,reason='efficiency_cap')
        if count and (not ready or now-s['changed']<45):return dict(decision,launch=False,reason='measuring_robust_step_windows')
        return decision
```

```
        log=(RUN/f'operations/{name}.log').open('a')
        p=subprocess.Popen([sys.executable,'-u',str(RUN/f'operations/{name}.py'),*args],env=env,

        records.append(dict(host='local4090',process=name,pid=p.pid))
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/operations/probe.py

- project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/operations/probe.py

SHA-256: 0998d6b3775c3e189bef08cd85a92b9177dbb097194d834e9ac86f939904211e

```
import subprocess
def command(args):
    return subprocess.check_output(args,text=True,timeout=15,stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)


def probe(own_pids=()):
    own=set(own_pids);gpus={}
    for line in command(['nvidia-smi','--query-gpu=index,uuid,name,memory.total,memory.free,utilization.gpu','--format=csv,noheader,nounits']).splitlines():
        idx,uuid,name,total,free,util=[s.strip() for s in line.split(',')]
        gpus[idx]=dict(uuid=uuid,name=name,total_mib=int(total),free_mib=int(free),utilization=int(util),other_pids=[],own_pids=[],process_memory_mib={},external_sm=0,process_utilization_known=True)
    uuids={g['uuid']:g for g in gpus.values()}
    for line in command(['nvidia-smi','--query-compute-apps=gpu_uuid,pid,used_memory','--format=csv,noheader,nounits']).splitlines():
        fields=[s.strip() for s in line.split(',')]
        if len(fields)!=3 or fields[0] not in uuids:continue
        uuid,pid,memory=fields;pid=int(pid);g=uuids[uuid]
        g['own_pids' if pid in own else 'other_pids'].append(pid)
        if memory.isdigit():g['process_memory_mib'][str(pid)]=int(memory)
    process_sm={}
    try:
        for line in command(['nvidia-smi','pmon','-c','1','-s','um']).splitlines():
            cols=line.split()
            if not cols or cols[0].startswith('#') or len(cols)<5:continue
            if cols[1].isdigit() and cols[3].isdigit():process_sm[(cols[0],int(cols[1]))]=int(cols[3])
    except (subprocess.SubprocessError,OSError):pass
    for idx,g in gpus.items():
        g['external_sm']=sum(process_sm.get((idx,pid),0) for pid in g['other_pids'])
        g['process_utilization_known']=all((idx,pid) in process_sm for pid in g['other_pids'])
    return gpus


```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/operations/report_diagnostics.py

- project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/operations/report_diagnostics.py

```
"""Persist the required diagnostic comparisons as soon as both groups are complete."""
from pathlib import Path
import json,sys,csv
RUN=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];sys.path.insert(0,str(RUN/'code'))
from settings import atomic_json,now,JOBS
from collect import records


def report():
    groups=records()
    for group in ['R0','TF_R3']:
        for seed in range(42,47):
            out=JOBS/f'REFERENCE_{group}_r{seed}'
            if (out/'SUCCESS.json').exists():groups[group][seed]=json.loads((out/'metrics.json').read_text())
    complete={g:values for g,values in groups.items() if set(values)==set(range(42,47)) and
              all('diagnostics' in v['metrics'] for v in values.values())}
    rows=[];diversity=[]
    def pooled(group,bin_name):
        bins=[complete[group][seed]['metrics']['diagnostics']['bins'][bin_name] for seed in range(42,47)]
        count=sum(v['count'] for v in bins)
        squared=sum(v['squared_error_sum'] for v in bins)
        cap=sum(v['capture_numerator'] for v in bins);den=sum(v['capture_denominator'] for v in bins)
        return count,squared/count if count else None,cap/den if den else None
    for group,values in complete.items():
        for ref in ['R0','TF_R3','TF_E2L','R2','R0+H','R3','R4-noS','R4-wrongS']:
            if group==ref or ref not in complete:continue
            for name in values[42]['metrics']['diagnostics']['bins']:
                n,x,cx=pooled(group,name);nr,y,cy=pooled(ref,name);assert n==nr
                rows.append(dict(candidate=group,reference=ref,bin=name,count=n,candidate_mse=x,reference_mse=y,
                                 delta_mse=x-y if n else None,mse_change_percent=100*(x/y-1) if n and y else None,
                                 candidate_capture_ratio=cx,reference_capture_ratio=cy))
        for seed,value in values.items():
            d=value['metrics']['diagnostics']
            if 'sample_std_mean' in d:
                diversity.append(dict(group=group,seed=seed,**{k:d[k] for k in ['sample_std_mean','nonzero_sample_std_fraction','sample_median_mae','sample_median_rmse']}))
    atomic_json(RUN/'analysis/DIAGNOSTICS.json',dict(timestamp=now(),complete_groups=sorted(complete),comparisons=rows,
                stochastic_diversity=diversity,note='Pooled per-seed diagnostic errors; not errors of an ensemble prediction. No selection by these diagnostics.'))
    if rows:
        with (RUN/'analysis/diagnostic_comparisons.csv').open('w') as f:
            w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(rows[0]));w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)

if __name__=='__main__':report()
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/operations/resource_limits.py

- project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/operations/resource_limits.py

SHA-256: 9d5fc2804d569af4e032d097d712fbecf2afb765c468f5d547ab326aa966c49c

```
"""Host and hierarchical cgroup memory headroom with pending-worker reservations."""
from pathlib import Path
import os,time,json
RUN=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

def memory_status():
    mem={line.split(':')[0]:int(line.split()[1])*1024 for line in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines() if line.startswith(('MemTotal:','MemAvailable:'))}
    available=mem['MemAvailable'];total=mem['MemTotal'];groups=[]
    for line in Path('/proc/self/cgroup').read_text().splitlines():
        if not line.startswith('0::'):continue
        start=Path('/sys/fs/cgroup')/line[3:].lstrip('/')
        for folder in [start,*start.parents]:
            if not folder.is_relative_to('/sys/fs/cgroup'):break
            limit=folder/'memory.max';current=folder/'memory.current'
            if not limit.exists() or not current.exists():continue
            raw=limit.read_text().strip()
            if raw=='max':continue
            ceiling=int(raw);used=int(current.read_text());statfile=folder/'memory.stat'
            stats=dict(line.split() for line in statfile.read_text().splitlines()) if statfile.exists() else {}
            reclaim=max(0,int(stats.get('inactive_file',0))-int(stats.get('file_dirty',0))-int(stats.get('file_writeback',0)))
            free=max(0,ceiling-used+reclaim);available=min(available,free);total=min(total,ceiling)
            groups.append(dict(path=str(folder),limit_mib=ceiling/2**20,current_mib=used/2**20,inactive_reclaimable_mib=reclaim/2**20,available_mib=free/2**20))
    # A worker not yet resident must not look free to simultaneous CPU/GPU dispatchers.
    uptime=float(Path('/proc/uptime').read_text().split()[0]);hz=os.sysconf('SC_CLK_TCK');reserved=0;pending=[]
    for folder in Path('/proc').iterdir():
        if not folder.name.isdigit():continue
        try:
            if folder.stat().st_uid!=os.getuid():continue
            cmd=(folder/'cmdline').read_bytes()
            if str(RUN.parents[1]/'runs').encode() not in cmd or b'--job-id' not in cmd:continue
            # Once this PID has completed an epoch, loading is over and its real
            # resident memory is already counted by the cgroup. Do not reserve
            # another3GiB throughout a short training job's first minute.
            parts=cmd.split(b'\0')
            if b'--job-id' in parts:
                jid=parts[parts.index(b'--job-id')+1].decode()
                progress=RUN/'jobs'/jid/'progress.json'
                if progress.exists():
                    try:
                        if json.loads(progress.read_text()).get('pid')==int(folder.name):continue
                    except (OSError,ValueError):pass
            stat=(folder/'stat').read_text().rsplit(')',1)[1].split();age=uptime-int(stat[19])/hz
            if age>=60 or stat[0] in ['Z','X']:continue
            row=next((x for x in (folder/'status').read_text().splitlines() if x.startswith('VmRSS:')),None)
            rss=int(row.split()[1])*1024 if row else 0
            amount=max(0,3*2**30-rss);reserved+=amount;pending.append(dict(pid=int(folder.name),age_seconds=age,reserved_mib=amount/2**20))
        except (OSError,ValueError,IndexError,ProcessLookupError):continue
    return dict(available_mib=max(0,available-reserved)/2**20,total_mib=total/2**20,pending_reserved_mib=reserved/2**20,cgroups=groups,pending_workers=pending)

def effective_memory_available():
    d=memory_status();return d['available_mib'],d['total_mib']
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/operations/scheduler.py
