```
"""Two workers per used GPU, with memory reserve, resumable OOM and bounded third-worker trials."""
from pathlib import Path
import sys,os,json,time,socket,subprocess,fcntl,argparse,collections,signal,math
RUN=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];sys.path.insert(0,str(RUN/'code'))
from settings import JOBS,atomic_json,now,sha
from probe import probe
from resource_limits import effective_memory_available
from storage_guard import storage_headroom
from efficiency_v3 import EfficiencyGovernor


def main(host):
    lock=(RUN/'schedulers/scheduler.lock').open('a+');fcntl.flock(lock,fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB)
    assert json.loads((RUN/'operations/CHECKS.json').read_text())['passed']
    for path,value in json.loads((RUN/'SCIENCE_FREEZE.json').read_text())['files'].items():assert sha(RUN/path)==value,path
    policy=json.loads((RUN/'operations/RESOURCES.json').read_text());pool=list(map(str,policy[host]['gpus']))
    governor=EfficiencyGovernor(JOBS,3);running={};failed=set();last=collections.defaultdict(float);cooldown=collections.defaultdict(float)
    assignments=json.loads((RUN/'operations/ASSIGNMENTS.json').read_text());stopping=False
    def stop(*args):
        nonlocal stopping
        stopping=True
    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM,stop);signal.signal(signal.SIGINT,stop)
    while True:
        for jid,item in list(running.items()):
            rc=item['p'].poll()
            if rc is None:continue
            item['lock'].close();item['log'].close();del running[jid]
            if rc==75:cooldown[item['gpu']]=time.monotonic()+30
            elif rc:failed.add(jid)
        stopping=stopping or (RUN/'operations/STOP.json').exists()
        specs=json.loads((RUN/'manifest.json').read_text())+json.loads((RUN/'diagnostic_manifest.json').read_text())
        eligible=[s for s in specs if assignments[s['id']]==host and all((RUN/d).exists() for d in s.get('dependencies',[])) and
                  not any((JOBS/s['id']/f).exists() for f in ['SUCCESS.json','FAILURE.json'])]
        # Concentrate short queues into pairs instead of one worker on many cards.
        used=[g for g in pool if any(x['gpu']==g for x in running.values())]
        limit=min(len(pool),max(1,len(eligible)//2))
        selected=used+[g for g in pool if g not in used][:max(0,limit-len(used))]
        try:
            gpus=probe(x['p'].pid for x in running.values());ram,totalram=effective_memory_available()
            storage=storage_headroom(RUN)
        except Exception as exc:
            atomic_json(RUN/'schedulers/status.json',dict(timestamp=now(),host=host,error=repr(exc)));time.sleep(10);continue
        decisions={}
        for gpu in sorted(selected,key=lambda g:sum(x['gpu']==g for x in running.values())):
            items=[(j,x) for j,x in running.items() if x['gpu']==gpu];g=gpus[gpu];count=len(items)
            reserve=max(policy['minimum_free_mib'],int(g['total_mib']*policy['memory_reserve_fraction']))
            observed=[g['process_memory_mib'].get(str(x['p'].pid),0) for _,x in items]
            budget=max(2304,int(max(observed,default=0)*1.3+384))
            ready=not any(x['p'].pid not in g['own_pids'] for _,x in items)
            decision=dict(launch=count<2,yield_count=0,reason='fill_pair',free_mib=g['free_mib'],reserve_mib=reserve,worker_budget_mib=budget)
            trial=governor.control(gpu,items,dict(decision,launch=count<3))
            if count>=2:
                decision=trial
                if decision.get('yield_count') and count<=2:decision.update(yield_count=0,launch=False)
            if g['free_mib']<reserve and items:
                decision.update(launch=False,yield_count=1,reason='memory_reserve')
            if stopping:decision.update(launch=False,yield_count=count,reason='cooperative_stop')
            if decision.get('yield_count'):
                for jid,item in sorted(items,key=lambda it:it[1]['started'],reverse=True)[:decision['yield_count']]:
                    if not item['diagnostic']:atomic_json(JOBS/jid/'YIELD_REQUEST.json',dict(timestamp=now(),reason=decision['reason']))
            if g['free_mib']<reserve+budget or not ready or time.monotonic()-last[gpu]<20 or time.monotonic()<cooldown[gpu]:
                decision.update(launch=False,reason='memory_or_warmup')
            if ram<max(8192,totalram*.1)+2048 or not storage['ok']:decision.update(launch=False,reason='host_memory_or_storage')
            if len(failed)>=2:decision.update(launch=False,reason='failure_circuit_breaker')
            decisions[gpu]=decision
            if not decision['launch']:continue
            for spec in eligible:
                jid=spec['id']
                if jid in running or jid in failed:continue
                out=JOBS/jid;out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);claim=(out/'worker.lock').open('a+')
                try:fcntl.flock(claim,fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB)
                except BlockingIOError:claim.close();continue
                (out/'YIELD_REQUEST.json').unlink(missing_ok=True)
                diagnostic=spec.get('task')=='reference_evaluation'
                entry='reference_evaluation.py' if diagnostic else 'train.py'
                env=dict(os.environ,CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=gpu,OMP_NUM_THREADS='1',MKL_NUM_THREADS='1',OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS='1',PYTHONNOUSERSITE='1')
                log=(out/'worker.log').open('a')
                p=subprocess.Popen([sys.executable,'-u',str(RUN/'code'/entry),'--job-id',jid],env=env,
                    stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,pass_fds=(claim.fileno(),))
                running[jid]=dict(p=p,lock=claim,log=log,gpu=gpu,started=time.monotonic(),diagnostic=diagnostic)
                last[gpu]=time.monotonic();ram-=2048
                print(now(),'launch',jid,'gpu',gpu,'workers',count+1,flush=True);break
        active=[dict(id=j,pid=x['p'].pid,gpu=x['gpu'],host=host) for j,x in running.items()]
        atomic_json(RUN/'schedulers/status.json',dict(timestamp=now(),host=host,pid=os.getpid(),active=active,
                    decisions=decisions,failed=sorted(failed),eligible=len(eligible),stopping=stopping,storage=storage))
        if not running and (stopping or len(failed)>=2):break
        time.sleep(8)

if __name__=='__main__':
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--host',required=True);main(p.parse_args().host)
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/operations/storage_guard.py

- project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/operations/storage_guard.py

SHA-256: 3944166ac1cbfcc603b638dc93e37b51fec7e9272c03c360666be9c43d283589

```
"""Include user quotas: df free space alone does not ensure writable output."""
from pathlib import Path
import os,shutil,subprocess

def storage_headroom(run):
    rows=[];quota_cache={}
    for label,path,reserve in [('metadata',Path(run),512*2**20),('jobs',Path(run)/'jobs',2*2**30),('data',Path(run)/'data',2*2**30)]:
        target=path.resolve();available=shutil.disk_usage(target).free;quota=None
        mount=next((m for m in ['/shared_data_ssd2','/shared_data_ssd3'] if target.is_relative_to(m)),None)
        if mount and shutil.which('lfs'):
            if mount not in quota_cache:
                out=subprocess.check_output(['lfs','quota','-u',str(os.getuid()),mount],text=True,timeout=15)
                line=next(x.split() for x in out.splitlines() if x.startswith(mount+' '))
                used=int(line[1].rstrip('*'));limit=int(line[3].rstrip('*'))
                quota_cache[mount]=max(0,limit-used)*1024 if limit else None
            quota=quota_cache[mount]
            if quota is not None:available=min(available,quota)
        rows.append(dict(label=label,path=str(target),available_mib=available/2**20,reserve_mib=reserve/2**20,quota_headroom_mib=quota/2**20 if quota is not None else None,ok=available>=reserve))
    return dict(ok=all(x['ok'] for x in rows),paths=rows)
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/ramp_information_diagnostic_20261115/README_CN.md

```
# 大变化信息来源诊断（2026-11-15，零训练，2023，55 站）

- `info.py`：实际变化 |Δ| > 0.3 的样本上，持续法、纯功率 B0（共享，5 seeds 平均）、W0_R3 / TF_R3（共享，5 seeds 平均）、Pangu 隐含变化、ERA5 隐含变化的捕获比例、相关、POD / FAR，以及模型残差与天气隐含变化的相关。
  - 天气隐含变化 = 2022 年拟合的 10 m 风速–功率分箱曲线 f，取 f(目标时刻风速) − f(起点风速)。
  - ERA5 取自 `privileged_wind_age_pilot` 的标签（已逆标准化），与每日协议起点逐一对齐。
- `shift.py`：用 10 m / 1000 hPa / 925 hPa 风速重算 4 h 时效；并把隐含变化平移 −2 至 +2 h，检验时间错位信号。
- `origin_err.py`：起点处 ERA5 − Pangu 的风速误差（本站与区域平均）能否解释 W0 残差。
- `state.py`：按当前功率档、变化方向、平台段统计天气隐含变化的误报率；时间平滑的作用；以及考虑运行状态的换算方式（截断到可变化空间、以当前实际功率为基准）。
```

```
    return o,np.asarray(h[:,:,-1]),np.asarray(y[:,:,LEADS]),wsP,wsE
o2,h2,y2,P2,E2=load('train'); o3,h3,y3,P3,E3=load('val')
print('ERA5 10m ws mean %.2f, Pangu %.2f (2023)'%(E3.mean(),P3.mean()))
B0=np.mean([np.asarray(np.load(f'{D}/reference_r{sd_}/val.npy',mmap_mode='r')[:,:,LEADS]) for sd_ in range(42,47)],0)
def curve_fit(ws,p):
    e=np.quantile(ws,np.linspace(0,1,41)); b=np.clip(np.searchsorted(e,ws)-1,0,39); m=np.array([p[b==i].mean() if (b==i).any() else np.nan for i in range(40)])
    m=np.where(np.isnan(m),np.nanmean(p),m); return lambda w: m[np.clip(np.searchsorted(e,w)-1,0,39)]
R={}
def add(k,v): R.setdefault(k,[]).append(v)
names=['persist','B0','W0','TF','Pangu','ERA5']
for s in range(55):
    # curves: 2022, weather-only, fitted per source on concurrent (valid-time) power: use lead pairs (ws at lead k, y at lead k) pooled
    fP=curve_fit(P2[:,s,1:].ravel(),y2[:,s].ravel()); fE=curve_fit(E2[:,s,1:].ravel(),y2[:,s].ravel())
    W0=np.mean([np.load(f'{FT}/jobs/W0_P1_FULL_s{s:02d}_r{x}/p0_prediction.npy')[:,0][:,LEADS] for x in range(42,47)],0)
    TF=np.mean([np.load(f'{FT}/jobs/TF_P1_FULL_s{s:02d}_r{x}/p0_prediction.npy')[:,0][:,LEADS] for x in range(42,47)],0)
    hl=h3[:,s][:,None]; dy=y3[:,s]-hl
    pred_change={'persist':np.zeros_like(dy),'B0':B0[:,s]-hl,'W0':W0-hl,'TF':TF-hl,
                 'Pangu':fP(P3[:,s,1:])-fP(P3[:,s,:1]),'ERA5':fE(E3[:,s,1:])-fE(E3[:,s,:1])}
    for j,L in enumerate(LEADS):
        d=dy[:,j]; ev=np.abs(d)>.3; add(('evfrac',L),ev.mean())
        for nme in names:
            pc=pred_change[nme][:,j]
            add((nme,L,'capture'),(np.sign(d)*pc)[ev].sum()/np.abs(d)[ev].sum())
            add((nme,L,'corr'),np.corrcoef(pc,d)[0,1] if pc.std()>0 else 0.)
            # event detection: POD/FAR with |pred change|>0.15 in same direction as event
            hit=(np.abs(pc)>.15)&(np.sign(pc)==np.sign(d))
            add((nme,L,'POD'),hit[ev].mean())
            fa=(np.abs(pc)>.15)&~((np.abs(d)>.15)&(np.sign(d)==np.sign(pc))); add((nme,L,'FAR'),fa.sum()/max((np.abs(pc)>.15).sum(),1))
        # unused info: correlation of model residual with weather-implied change, on all and on events
        for m,pm in [('W0',W0),('TF',TF)]:
            res=y3[:,s,j]-pm[:,j]
            for src in ['Pangu','ERA5']:
                add((m,src,L,'rescorr'),np.corrcoef(res,pred_change[src][:,j])[0,1])
M=lambda *k: np.mean(R[k])
for L in LEADS:
    print(f'\n== lead {(L+1)/4:.0f} h: share of samples with |Δ|>0.3: {M("evfrac",L):.3%}')
    print('   source    capture(|Δ|>.3)  corr(predΔ,Δ)   POD(|predΔ|>.15 same dir)  FAR')
    for nme in names: print(f'   {nme:8s}  {M(nme,L,"capture"):9.3f}  {M(nme,L,"corr"):12.3f}  {M(nme,L,"POD"):14.3f}  {M(nme,L,"FAR"):14.3f}')
    for m in ['W0','TF']: print(f'   corr(residual of {m}, weather-implied Δ): Pangu {M(m,"Pangu",L,"rescorr"):+.3f}  ERA5 {M(m,"ERA5",L,"rescorr"):+.3f}')
```

```
"""If ERA5 up to the origin is available: does the forecast error at the origin (ERA5 - Pangu, 10 m wind at the base hour <= origin)
explain W0's remaining 0-6 h error? Own-farm and 55-farm regional mean. 2023, zero training."""
import numpy as np, json, sys
sys.path.insert(0,'/shared_data_ssd2/liying/wind_cluster_project_20261018/project/runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/code')
P='/shared_data_ssd2/liying/wind_cluster_project_20261018/project/runs'; D=f'{P}/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/data'; S=f'{P}/privileged_wind_age_pilot_20261101_v1/data'; FT=f'{P}/daily_site_finetune_20261114_v1'; A=f'{P}/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/assets'
mu=np.load(f'{A}/nwp_mean.npy'); sd=np.load(f'{A}/nwp_std.npy'); emu=np.load(f'{S}/cache/static/nwp_mean.npy')[1:3]; esd=np.load(f'{S}/cache/static/nwp_std.npy')[1:3]
keep=np.array(json.load(open(f'{A}/COHORT.json'))['original_indices'])
cy=np.load(f'{D}/val/cycle.npy'); cy2=np.load(f'{S}/common/val/cycle.npy'); off=np.load(f'{D}/val/offset.npy')
nodes=np.load(f'{D}/val/nodes.npy',mmap_mode='r'); era=np.load(f'{S}/labels/val/future_uv.npy',mmap_mode='r')
y=np.asarray(np.load(f'{D}/val/y.npy',mmap_mode='r')); 
LEADS=[3,7,15,23]
# node 0 is valid at base hour (<= origin). ERA5 node j for j>0 is future -> only node 0 used as "available analysis".
pg=np.hypot(*(np.asarray(nodes[cy][:,:,0,[1,2]])*sd[[1,2]]+mu[[1,2]]).transpose(2,0,1))
er=np.asarray(era[cy2][:,keep,0])*esd+emu; er=np.hypot(er[...,0],er[...,1])
err0=er-pg; reg=err0.mean(1,keepdims=True).repeat(55,1)
# future forecast error at target (for reference: how persistent is the 10 m error?)
R={}
for s in range(55):
    W0=np.mean([np.load(f'{FT}/jobs/W0_P1_FULL_s{s:02d}_r{x}/p0_prediction.npy')[:,0] for x in range(42,47)],0)
    for L in LEADS:
        res=y[:,s,L]-W0[:,L]
        R.setdefault((L,'own'),[]).append(np.corrcoef(res,err0[:,s])[0,1]); R.setdefault((L,'reg'),[]).append(np.corrcoef(res,reg[:,s])[0,1])
        X=np.stack([np.ones(len(res)),err0[:,s],reg[:,s]],1); half=len(res)//2
        b=np.linalg.lstsq(X[:half],res[:half],rcond=None)[0]; r2=res[half:]-X[half:]@b
        R.setdefault((L,'mse_gain'),[]).append((r2**2).mean()/(res[half:]**2).mean()-1); R.setdefault((L,'mae_gain'),[]).append(np.abs(r2).mean()/np.abs(res[half:]).mean()-1)
for L in LEADS: print(f'lead {(L+1)/4:.0f}h: corr(W0 residual, own ERA5−Pangu at origin) {np.mean(R[(L,"own")]):+.3f}; regional mean {np.mean(R[(L,"reg")]):+.3f}; linear correction (fit H1 2023, eval H2) MSE {100*np.mean(R[(L,"mse_gain")]):+.2f}% MAE {100*np.mean(R[(L,"mae_gain")]):+.2f}%')
```

```
import numpy as np, json, sys

def ws(sp,qs):
    cy=np.load(f'{D}/{sp}/cycle.npy'); off=np.load(f'{D}/{sp}/offset.npy'); nodes=np.load(f'{D}/{sp}/nodes.npy',mmap_mode='r')

    return lambda w:m[np.clip(np.searchsorted(e,w)-1,0,39)]

    for k in CH:

        base=f(W3[k][:,s,0])
        for j,sh in zip(range(1,6),[-2,-1,0,1,2]):
            imp=f(W3[k][:,s,j])-base
            R.setdefault((k,sh,'res'),[]).append(np.corrcoef(res,imp)[0,1]); R.setdefault((k,sh,'resev'),[]).append(np.corrcoef(res[ev],imp[ev])[0,1])
            R.setdefault((k,sh,'dy'),[]).append(np.corrcoef(d,imp)[0,1])
for k in CH:
```

```
"""Why weather-implied large changes often do not appear in power: operating point, plateau (possible curtailment), direction, short-lived weather changes.
2023, 4 h lead, ERA5 (true weather) and Pangu; zero training."""
import numpy as np
exec(open(__import__('os').path.join(__import__('os').path.dirname(__file__),'info.py')).read().split("R={}")[0])
L=15; j=LEADS.index(L)
# extra queries for smoothing: implied change using mean of ws over [3h,5h] vs point at 4h
def ws_at(sp,qs):
    import sys
    o=np.load(f'{D}/{sp}/origin.npy'); cy2=np.load(f'{S}/common/{sp}/cycle.npy'); off=np.load(f'{D}/{sp}/offset.npy')
    era=np.load(f'{S}/labels/{sp}/future_uv.npy',mmap_mode='r'); out=np.zeros((len(o),55,len(qs)),'f4')
    for s in range(55):
        e=(np.asarray(era[cy2,keep[s]])*esd+emu).astype('f4'); w=interpolate_nodes(e,off,qs); out[:,s]=np.hypot(w[...,0],w[...,1])
    return out
Esm=ws_at('val',np.array([3.,3.5,4.,4.5,5.]))
H3=np.asarray(np.load(f'{D}/val/h.npy',mmap_mode='r'))
rows=[]
for s in range(55):
    fE=curve_fit(E2[:,s,1:].ravel(),y2[:,s].ravel()); fP=curve_fit(P2[:,s,1:].ravel(),y2[:,s].ravel())
    hl=h3[:,s]; d=y3[:,s,j]-hl
    impE=fE(E3[:,s,1+j])-fE(E3[:,s,0]); impP=fP(P3[:,s,1+j])-fP(P3[:,s,0]); impEs=fE(Esm[:,s]).mean(1)-fE(E3[:,s,0])
    hist=H3[:,s]; flat=(np.ptp(hist[:,-8:],1)<0.01)&(hl>0.05)&(hl<0.95)   # 2 h nearly constant, mid range: plateau (possible curtailment / limit)
    lvl=np.digitize(hl,[.1,.3,.7])
    rows.append((d,impE,impP,impEs,lvl,flat))
def far(imp,d,mask):
    sig=(np.abs(imp)>.15)&mask; ok=(np.abs(d)>.15)&(np.sign(d)==np.sign(imp)); return (sig&~ok).sum()/max(sig.sum(),1), sig.sum()
def agg(fn):
    a=np.array([fn(*r) for r in rows]); return a
print('4 h lead. FAR = share of weather-implied |Δ|>0.15 signals NOT followed by same-direction power change >0.15')
for name,k in [('ERA5',1),('Pangu',2),('ERA5 smoothed 3-5h',3)]:
    tot=[far(r[k],r[0],np.ones_like(r[0],bool)) for r in rows]; print(f'  {name:20s} all: FAR {np.sum([t[0]*t[1] for t in tot])/np.sum([t[1] for t in tot]):.3f}')
print('  ERA5 by current power level and direction (FAR, signal count):')
for lv,lab in enumerate(['<0.1','0.1-0.3','0.3-0.7','>=0.7']):
    for sgn,sl in [(1,'up'),(-1,'down')]:
        t=[far(r[1],r[0],(r[4]==lv)&(np.sign(r[1])==sgn)) for r in rows]; n=np.sum([x[1] for x in t])
        print(f'     level {lab:8s} {sl:4s}: FAR {np.sum([x[0]*x[1] for x in t])/max(n,1):.3f}  n={n}')
t=[far(r[1],r[0],r[5]) for r in rows]; n=np.sum([x[1] for x in t]); t2=[far(r[1],r[0],~r[5]) for r in rows]; n2=np.sum([x[1] for x in t2])
print(f'  ERA5 when last 2 h power is flat at mid level (plateau): FAR {np.sum([x[0]*x[1] for x in t])/max(n,1):.3f} n={n};  otherwise {np.sum([x[0]*x[1] for x in t2])/max(n2,1):.3f} n={n2}')
# magnitude attenuation: actual / implied for true signals
ratio=[]; 
for d,iE,_,_,lv,_ in rows:
    m=np.abs(iE)>.3; ratio.append(np.sum(d[m]*np.sign(iE[m]))/np.sum(np.abs(iE[m])))
print(f'  On ERA5-implied |Δ|>0.3: realized change in that direction / implied magnitude = {np.mean(ratio):.3f}')

print('\n  State-aware conversions of the same ERA5 / Pangu weather (4 h):')
for src,k in [('ERA5',None),('Pangu',None)]:
    acc={'curve change':[], 'anchored+clipped (headroom)':[], 'level: f(future)-current power':[]}
    for s,(d,iE,iP,_,lv,_) in enumerate(rows):
        hl=h3[:,s]
        if src=='ERA5': fE=curve_fit(E2[:,s,1:].ravel(),y2[:,s].ravel()); fut=fE(E3[:,s,1+j]); imp=iE
        else: fP=curve_fit(P2[:,s,1:].ravel(),y2[:,s].ravel()); fut=fP(P3[:,s,1+j]); imp=iP
        cand={'curve change':imp,'anchored+clipped (headroom)':np.clip(hl+imp,0,1)-hl,'level: f(future)-current power':fut-hl}
        for nme,c in cand.items():
            sig=np.abs(c)>.15; ok=(np.abs(d)>.15)&(np.sign(d)==np.sign(c)); ev=np.abs(d)>.3
            acc[nme].append(((sig&~ok).sum(),sig.sum(),(hit:=(sig&ok&ev)).sum(),ev.sum(),np.corrcoef(c,d)[0,1]))
    for nme,v in acc.items():
        v=np.array(v); print(f'     {src:5s} {nme:32s} FAR {v[:,0].sum()/v[:,1].sum():.3f}  POD(|Δ|>.3) {v[:,2].sum()/v[:,3].sum():.3f}  corr {v[:,4].mean():.3f}')
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/rmse_gap_diagnostic_20261115/README_CN.md

- project/runs/rmse_gap_diagnostic_20261115/README_CN.md

```
# W0 与 TF 的 RMSE 差距诊断（2026-11-15，零训练）

数据：`daily_site_finetune_20261114_v1` 保存的共享模型预测（`p0_prediction.npy`，即 W0_R3 / TF_R3）。55 站 × 5 seeds，2023 年，每日协议。只读 CPU，不训练，不读 2024。

- `diag.py`：误差分布、预测与观测的回归斜率、按实际变化幅度 |y − 当前功率| 分解 ΔMSE / ΔMAE，以及 W0 与 TF 平均。
- `ens.py`：5-seed 集成后的差距，以及 seed 方差占 MSE 的比例。
- `ramp.py`：按"预测变化 × 实际变化"二维分箱的 ΔMSE；实际变化 > 0.3 时，模型捕获的变化比例。

主要结论见对话记录与 `HISTORICAL_WEATHER_AND_W0_PLAN` 之后的分析。
- `phase.py`：同一时效段内，目标落在整点与 :15 / :30 / :45 时的 MSE 对比（检验线性插值是否造成小时内部的损失）。
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/rmse_gap_diagnostic_20261115/diag.py

- project/runs/rmse_gap_diagnostic_20261115/diag.py

SHA-256: dbd897a1ddf1280ab2a2369b9c4cf3c6cfd5231d66db701dc851f202be2c0dd6

```
import numpy as np, json, os
R='/shared_data_ssd2/liying/wind_cluster_project_20261018/project/runs/daily_site_finetune_20261114_v1'
seeds=[42,43,44,45,46]; F=55
G=[('0-1h',0,4),('1-3h',4,12),('3-6h',12,24)]
acc={}
def add(k,v): acc.setdefault(k,[]).append(v)
rampb=[0,.05,.15,.3,1.01]
for s in range(F):
    y=np.load(f'{R}/feature_cache/s{s:02d}/val/y.npy').astype('f8'); h=np.load(f'{R}/feature_cache/s{s:02d}/val/h.npy')[:,-1].astype('f8')
    ramp=np.abs(y-h[:,None])
    for sd in seeds:
        P={m:np.load(f'{R}/jobs/{m}_P1_FULL_s{s:02d}_r{sd}/p0_prediction.npy')[:,0].astype('f8') for m in ['W0','TF']}
        P['AVG']=(P['W0']+P['TF'])/2
        for m,p in P.items():
            e=p-y
            add((m,'mae'),abs(e).mean()); add((m,'rmse'),np.sqrt((e**2).mean()))
            add((m,'bias'),e.mean()); add((m,'q99abs'),np.quantile(abs(e),.99))
            add((m,'tail_share'),(e[abs(e)>.3]**2).sum()/(e**2).sum())
            for g,a,b in G:
                ee=e[:,a:b]; pp=p[:,a:b]; yy=y[:,a:b]
                add((m,g,'mse'),(ee**2).mean()); add((m,g,'mae'),abs(ee).mean())
                add((m,g,'predstd'),pp.std()); add((m,g,'ystd'),yy.std())
                c=np.cov(pp.ravel(),yy.ravel()); add((m,g,'slope'),c[0,1]/c[0,0])   # y on pred; <1 => over-responsive
                for i in range(4):
                    k=(ramp[:,a:b]>=rampb[i])&(ramp[:,a:b]<rampb[i+1])
                    add((m,g,'sse_ramp',i),(ee[k]**2).sum()/ee.size); add((m,g,'ae_ramp',i),abs(ee[k]).sum()/ee.size)
                hp=h>=.75; add((m,g,'hp_mse'),(ee[hp]**2).mean())
                # mean minus median residual (MAE-loss median vs RMSE mean gap), pooled
                add((m,g,'mean_minus_median'),np.mean(-ee)-np.median(-ee))
M=lambda *k: np.mean(acc[k])
print('macro MAE/RMSE: W0 %.6f/%.6f  TF %.6f/%.6f  AVG %.6f/%.6f'%(M('W0','mae'),M('W0','rmse'),M('TF','mae'),M('TF','rmse'),M('AVG','mae'),M('AVG','rmse')))
for m in ['W0','TF']: print(m,'bias %.5f  q99|e| %.4f  share of SSE from |e|>0.3: %.3f'%(M(m,'bias'),M(m,'q99abs'),M(m,'tail_share')))
for g,_,_ in G:
    print(f'== {g}: W0/TF MSE ratio {M("W0",g,"mse")/M("TF",g,"mse")-1:+.3%}, MAE ratio {M("W0",g,"mae")/M("TF",g,"mae")-1:+.3%}, highpower MSE ratio {M("W0",g,"hp_mse")/M("TF",g,"hp_mse")-1:+.3%}')
    for m in ['W0','TF']: print(f'   {m}: pred std {M(m,g,"predstd"):.4f} (obs {M(m,g,"ystd"):.4f}), slope y~pred {M(m,g,"slope"):.3f}, mean-median residual {M(m,g,"mean_minus_median"):+.4f}')
    dS=[M('W0',g,'sse_ramp',i)-M('TF',g,'sse_ramp',i) for i in range(4)]; dA=[M('W0',g,'ae_ramp',i)-M('TF',g,'ae_ramp',i) for i in range(4)]
    tS=sum(dS); tA=sum(dA)
    print('   W0-TF ΔMSE by |y-h_last| bins [0,.05,.15,.3,1]:', ' '.join(f'{x:+.2e}' for x in dS), ' total %+.2e'%tS)
    print('   W0-TF ΔMAE by same bins             :', ' '.join(f'{x:+.2e}' for x in dA), ' total %+.2e'%tA)
```

```
import numpy as np
R='/shared_data_ssd2/liying/wind_cluster_project_20261018/project/runs/daily_site_finetune_20261114_v1'
seeds=[42,43,44,45,46]; F=55; G=[('all',0,24),('0-1h',0,4),('1-3h',4,12),('3-6h',12,24)]
res={}
for s in range(F):
    y=np.load(f'{R}/feature_cache/s{s:02d}/val/y.npy').astype('f8'); h=np.load(f'{R}/feature_cache/s{s:02d}/val/h.npy')[:,-1].astype('f8')
    P={m:np.stack([np.load(f'{R}/jobs/{m}_P1_FULL_s{s:02d}_r{sd}/p0_prediction.npy')[:,0] for sd in seeds]).astype('f8') for m in ['W0','TF']}
    cand={'W0_single':P['W0'],'TF_single':P['TF'],'W0_ens5':P['W0'].mean(0,keepdims=True),'TF_ens5':P['TF'].mean(0,keepdims=True),'W0TF_ens10':np.concatenate([P['W0'],P['TF']]).mean(0,keepdims=True)}
    for k,p in cand.items():
        e=p-y[None]
        for g,a,b in G:
            res.setdefault((k,g,'mae'),[]).append(np.abs(e[:,:,a:b]).mean(axis=(1,2)).mean())
            res.setdefault((k,g,'rmse'),[]).append(np.sqrt((e[:,:,a:b]**2).mean(axis=(1,2))).mean())
    # variance share: mean over seeds of MSE = MSE(ens) + mean seed variance
    for m in ['W0','TF']:
        e=P[m]-y[None]; v=P[m].var(0).mean(); res.setdefault((m,'var_share'),[]).append(v/(e**2).mean())
M=lambda *k: np.mean(res[k])
for g,_,_ in G:
    print(g)
    for k in ['W0_single','TF_single','W0_ens5','TF_ens5','W0TF_ens10']: print(f'   {k:11s} MAE {M(k,g,"mae"):.6f} RMSE {M(k,g,"rmse"):.6f}')
    print(f'   W0 vs TF single: {M("W0_single",g,"mae")/M("TF_single",g,"mae")-1:+.3%} / {M("W0_single",g,"rmse")/M("TF_single",g,"rmse")-1:+.3%};  ens5 vs ens5: {M("W0_ens5",g,"mae")/M("TF_ens5",g,"mae")-1:+.3%} / {M("W0_ens5",g,"rmse")/M("TF_ens5",g,"rmse")-1:+.3%}')
for m in ['W0','TF']: print(m,'seed-variance share of MSE: %.3f'%M(m,'var_share'))
```

```
"""Error at on-the-hour vs sub-hour targets (same lead block), W0_R3 & TF_R3 shared P0, 2023, 5 seeds mean. Zero training."""
import numpy as np
R='/shared_data_ssd2/liying/wind_cluster_project_20261018/project/runs/daily_site_finetune_20261114_v1'
D='/shared_data_ssd2/liying/wind_cluster_project_20261018/project/runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/data'
o=np.load(f'{D}/val/origin.npy'); minute=((o//60)%60)//15   # origin quarter
k=np.arange(24); tq=(minute[:,None]+k[None]+1)%4             # target quarter: 0 = on the hour
G=[('0-1h',0,4),('1-3h',4,12),('3-6h',12,24)]
acc={}
for s in range(55):
    y=np.load(f'{R}/feature_cache/s{s:02d}/val/y.npy').astype('f8'); h=np.load(f'{R}/feature_cache/s{s:02d}/val/h.npy')[:,-1].astype('f8')
    for m in ['W0','TF']:
        p=np.mean([np.load(f'{R}/jobs/{m}_P1_FULL_s{s:02d}_r{x}/p0_prediction.npy')[:,0] for x in range(42,47)],0)
        e2=(p-y)**2; ae=np.abs(p-y); dy2=(y-h[:,None])**2
        for g,a,b in G:
            for q in range(4):
                msk=(tq[:,a:b]==q)
                acc.setdefault((m,g,q,'mse'),[]).append(e2[:,a:b][msk].mean()); acc.setdefault((m,g,q,'mae'),[]).append(ae[:,a:b][msk].mean())
                acc.setdefault((g,q,'var'),[]).append(dy2[:,a:b][msk].mean())
for g,_,_ in G:
    print(g)
    for m in ['W0','TF']:
        base=np.mean(acc[(m,g,0,'mse')])
        print(f'  {m}: MSE by target minute :00/:15/:30/:45 = '+' '.join(f'{np.mean(acc[(m,g,q,"mse")]):.5f}' for q in range(4))+'   rel to :00 '+' '.join(f'{np.mean(acc[(m,g,q,"mse")])/base-1:+.2%}' for q in range(4)))
    vb=np.mean(acc[(g,0,'var')]); print('  E[(y-h)^2] (difficulty) rel to :00 '+' '.join(f'{np.mean(acc[(g,q,"var")])/vb-1:+.2%}' for q in range(4)))
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/rmse_gap_diagnostic_20261115/ramp.py

- project/runs/rmse_gap_diagnostic_20261115/ramp.py

SHA-256: 8f540ffcf5200cb09282b0dc3ad1a0970661bb684cda122baf036c8f6008b561

```
import numpy as np
R='/shared_data_ssd2/liying/wind_cluster_project_20261018/project/runs/daily_site_finetune_20261114_v1'
seeds=[42,43,44,45,46]; F=55; b=[0,.05,.15,.3,1.01]; nb=4
for g,a,c in [('1-3h',4,12),('3-6h',12,24)]:
    dM=np.zeros((nb,nb)); cnt=np.zeros((nb,nb)); cap={'W0':[0,0],'TF':[0,0]}; tot=0
    for s in range(F):
        y=np.load(f'{R}/feature_cache/s{s:02d}/val/y.npy')[:,a:c].astype('f8'); h=np.load(f'{R}/feature_cache/s{s:02d}/val/h.npy')[:,-1:].astype('f8')
        P={m:np.stack([np.load(f'{R}/jobs/{m}_P1_FULL_s{s:02d}_r{sd}/p0_prediction.npy')[:,0,a:c] for sd in seeds]).mean(0).astype('f8') for m in ['W0','TF']}
        dy=y-h; ia=np.clip(np.digitize(abs(dy),b)-1,0,nb-1)
        ref=(P['W0']+P['TF'])/2-h; ip=np.clip(np.digitize(abs(ref),b)-1,0,nb-1)   # predicted-change bin from the pair mean (same for both)
        dmse=(P['W0']-y)**2-(P['TF']-y)**2
        for i in range(nb):
            for j in range(nb):
                k=(ip==i)&(ia==j); dM[i,j]+=dmse[k].sum(); cnt[i,j]+=k.sum()
        tot+=y.size
        big=abs(dy)>.3
        for m in ['W0','TF']:
            cap[m][0]+=((P[m]-h)*np.sign(dy))[big].sum(); cap[m][1]+=abs(dy)[big].sum()
    print(g,'W0−TF ΔMSE (×1e-4 of per-sample MSE), rows: predicted |change| bin, cols: actual |change| bin [0,.05,.15,.3,1]')
    for i in range(nb): print('   pred',b[i],' '.join(f'{1e4*dM[i,j]/tot:+7.3f}' for j in range(nb)),'  n%:',' '.join(f'{100*cnt[i,j]/tot:5.1f}' for j in range(nb)))
    for m in ['W0','TF']: print(f'   {m}: on |actual change|>0.3, fraction of change captured in correct direction = {cap[m][0]/cap[m][1]:.3f}')
```
